# Every advancement in Too Many Hands

Legend: `·` task · `◆` goal · `★` challenge · `★★` super challenge

None of these duplicates any of the 1,776 advancements in BlazeandCave's Advancements Pack or Enhanced Discoveries, nor any of the others listed here.

---

# Too Many Hands

Advancements that cannot be earned alone. Every one needs between two and five players.

**855 advancements** across 15 tabs, plus 16 progression entries.

| Tab | Count |
|---|---|
| Adventure | 75 |
| Animals | 47 |
| Biomes | 56 |
| Building | 49 |
| Enchanting | 34 |
| Farming | 42 |
| Mining | 49 |
| Monsters | 26 |
| Nether | 106 |
| Potions | 85 |
| Redstone | 24 |
| Statistics | 14 |
| Super Challenges | 20 |
| The End | 89 |
| Weaponry | 139 |

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Allay Express** — Pure criteria, no function. 'courier' is allay_drop_item_on_block with exactly the location shape vanilla's Birthday Song and Enhanced Discoveries' Uncontrolled Reproduction use - a location_check on minecraft:note_block plus a match_tool on the carried item, here Paper. 'posted' is the Paper leaving your hand into another player's, with the distance-min-1 thrower guard. DELIBERATE CHANGE: the two halves are not linked to each other. The original wanted the Allay's delivery itself picked up by a second player, which needs a UUID match in a function; worse, I am not confident an Allay-dropped ItemEntity carries a player thrower at all, and if it does not, thrown_item_picked_up_by_player would never fire on the receiver and the advancement would be dead. So the receiving half is a plain hand-over of Paper instead, which is guaranteed to work. What the pack sees is 'you ran an Allay post office and you handed post to somebody' - unlinked, like Pay It Forward's two halves.
- **Bedside Manner** — Pure criteria, no function. 'cured' is the bare cured_zombie_villager trigger BACAP's Global Vaccination uses (there with a VillagerData nbt filter; unfiltered here, any profession will do). 'supplied' is a plain Golden Apple - not an Enchanted one, which is what the three existing hand-me-an-apple entries in this pack use - taken off another player's drop with the distance-min-1 guard, and it is the half that cannot be done alone. DELIBERATE CHANGE: the original had the OTHER player trade with the cured villager inside a minute, which needs the villager UUID stored and a timer. Here the doctor supplies neither the apple nor the customer: he takes the apple from a partner and trades with the patient himself. Nothing ties the trade to the villager that was cured, and there is no time window.
- **Care Package** — Four minecraft:thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity criteria, one per Iron piece, each requiring the picker to be a player at least one block from the thrower - the self-exclusion idiom from mining/diamonds_to_you, because this trigger has none of its own and a dropped item you walk back onto fires it at distance 0. requirements is an AND of the four, so all four pieces must reach somebody else's hands. The ten-second window in the original idea is dropped: a timer would have cost a detection function and the four-piece AND already cannot be done alone.
- **Closing Time** — Pure criteria, no function. 'bought' is villager_trade with BOTH the villager and item conditions, exactly as BACAP's Florist and Rebuilding the Oceans pin a Wandering Trader's specific stock; Packed Ice and Blue Ice are both listed so the criterion survives whichever of the two the trader happens to be offering. 'funded' and 'delivered' are the two directions of the hand-over idiom with the distance-min-1 guard, and either one alone is impossible solo. DELIBERATE CHANGE: 'the same trader, before he despawns' needs the trader's UUID matched across two players in a function. The despawn timer is not read at all - what makes this feel like closing time is that the ice has to be bought and passed on, not how long the trader lives. UNVERIFIED: I have not read the trade tables in this exact version; if a Wandering Trader in 1.21.9 offers neither ice, 'bought' is unattainable and the entry should be repointed at coral blocks, which BACAP already relies on in Rebuilding the Oceans.
- **Diamond Hands** — One vanilla criterion, the exact shape of BACAP's mining/diamonds_to_you but with the receiver pinned to entity_type minecraft:player and the stack pinned to count 64, so a mob picking it up and a single loose diamond both miss. The distance absolute min 1 guard is what excludes the thrower picking his own drop back up, since he is always at distance 0 from himself. Raised from the original one-diamond idea because 'Diamonds to you!' already covers throwing a diamond at another player or mob; a whole stack and a player-only receiver is what makes it a new advancement.
- **Distraction Tactics** — No detector. `rang` copies Enhanced Discoveries adventure/din_don exactly - default_block_use with a location_check on minecraft:bell - which is how a bare-handed bell ring is caught. The pairing runs in the FAST bucket, once per tick rather than every ten, because the rescued player's health is read live and half a second is long enough for them to heal or die. At the ringer's position it requires at least one #minecraft:raiders entity within 30 blocks (the same entity tag BACAP's own sound_the_alarm check uses, and the selector is radius-bounded), then grants to every non-spectator 1..30 blocks away sitting at 5 or less on the declared bacaptmh_m1hp health objective, then to the ringer, then clears the hook. distance=1..30 excludes the ringer, so it takes two players. Honest limit: the raiders' Target field is not read - what is enforced is a bell rung with raiders in range while a teammate is genuinely under three hearts, not a proven change of aggro.
- **Dress Code** — Every 5 s the armour material is read with `execute if items` over the four armour slots and the trim material is read from the equipment NBT of the same four slots, giving each player a material index and a trim index. Only `equipment.head/chest/legs/feet` is read: that is the attested player layout at this pack format - bacaped:animal/youre_part_of_a_hive_mind_now tests player NBT as Inventory slots 0-35 plus an `equipment` compound - so the old Inventory slot 100-103 fallback was dead code and doubled the cost of the scan for nothing. Three players inside 16 blocks who share both indices are granted; the proximity is what makes it a dress code rather than three strangers in three dimensions. Live reads replace advancement criteria, so players who already hold it keep counting towards everyone else's three. It keys on trim material, not pattern, exactly as BACAP's own Coordinated Flair does.
- **Drop Shipping** — No function needed after all: the distance predicate inside entity is measured from the thrower, so distance.y min 100 means the receiver must be a hundred blocks of vertical separation away at the moment he picks the parcel up — the thrower has to still be up the tower when it lands. The y field is an absolute difference (Enhanced Discoveries uses it the same way in adventure/nobody_needs_rockets), so a receiver a hundred blocks ABOVE also counts; that is a strictly harder way to earn it and I left it in rather than adding a function to forbid it. Any item qualifies, since a hundred blocks of vertical separation is not something two players hit by accident.
- **Dying Wish** — Pure criteria, no detector. 'gave' is minecraft:thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity, the trigger bacaptmh:adventure/hot_potato already uses, restricted to a player collector at distance min 1 so items you drop and re-collect never count. 'killed' is minecraft:entity_killed_player in the shape of blazeandcave:adventure/rekt, with the killer pinned to a player at distance min 1 — that distance guard matters here because entity_killed_player has no self-exclusion and a projectile of your own coming back down would otherwise satisfy it. Honest limitations, stated plainly: the 'entire inventory' and 'within fifteen seconds' parts of the idea are both dropped. I had no verified example of an empty-inventory slot predicate to copy, and ordering needs a detector. On a busy PvP server the two criteria can therefore be met incidentally rather than as a deliberate ritual.
- **Escort Mission** — Function-granted. bacaptmh_walkd is the player's walk_one_cm + sprint_one_cm delta for the window; both mirrors are seeded in seed_lines and both deltas are rebuilt for EVERY player, ungated, at the top of bucket_medium, so a player the gate has never touched can never have a lifetime statistic read as one window of travel. detect/escort then runs only for non-spectator, non-creative players who lack the advancement: if the head slot is not a Carved Pumpkin, or a real head count of non-spectators within 5 blocks is under 2, bacaptmh_esc is zeroed and the run returns, so any break in the escort restarts the tally from zero. Otherwise a delta inside 1..2000 cm (positive, and still physically possible on foot in half a second, which also discards the stale mirror a gamemode round trip leaves behind) is added; at 20000 cm the advancement is granted to every non-spectator within 5 blocks, guide included. The guide is not required to be the same player throughout - any escort within 5 blocks keeps the tally alive.
- **Even Split** — Collapsed to one atomic criterion instead of the two the idea sketched: at the instant the other player picks up the drop, the thrown stack must be exactly 32 gold ingots and the thrower's own mainhand must hold exactly 32 more, read through the same player.equipment.mainhand predicate BACAP uses in weaponry/backfire. Both halves are therefore checked at the same tick with no timing window to get wrong. The one way to miss is dropping the 32 onto other loose gold ingots, because item entities merge and the stack on the ground would no longer be exactly 32.
- **Fair Trade** — `gave` fires when an item you threw is picked up by another player and `got` fires when you pick up an item another player threw; both carry `entity.distance.absolute.min = 1`, the guard BACAP uses in mining/diamonds_to_you, so picking your own dropped item back up (distance 0) latches neither. Every 0.5 s the detector stamps a 5-second timer on each flag and revokes it, and grants to any player holding both timers at once - which means an item of theirs went to somebody else and somebody else's item came to them inside the same window. Only the claimant needs live criteria, so a partner who already owns the advancement can still trade with a newcomer. Both timers are cleared on the grant.
- **Ferryman** — Function-granted by detect/ferry (mode 1), dispatched every medium tick as every non-spectator, non-creative player who is missing it. The detector takes that player's boat_one_cm delta against a private mirror (bacaptmh_boatm, seeded in seed.mcfunction the first time the pack sees a player, so a lifetime total can never read as one window's travel and the delta is additionally capped at 20000 cm per window). It then walks 'on vehicle ... on passengers' - players are never written into a vehicle's Passengers NBT, so the walk is the only reliable way to see who else is aboard - and looks for a passenger who is not the rower (excluded with a per-call tag, not with a distance test), whose bacaptmh_dtime death stamp is newer than 12000 ticks, and who fails 'if items' on container.*, all four armour slots and weapon.offhand, i.e. is carrying literally nothing. While such a passenger is aboard the delta accumulates into bacaptmh_ferry; the moment the condition breaks the accumulator is reset to 0, so the thousand blocks must be one unbroken ride. At 100000 cm every non-spectator player in that boat is granted, rower and rescued alike. Honest limits: the ride does not have to be in a straight line or end anywhere in particular, and a passenger who dies, empties themselves deliberately and climbs in qualifies just as well as a genuine rescue.
- **Fire Sale** — thrown_item_picked_up_by_player puts the receiver in the player field and the thrower in entity, so the burning man is the achiever and flags.is_on_fire is read off him exactly as BACAP reads it in weaponry/backfire. The idea's second leg — actually putting yourself out with the bucket — was deliberately dropped: buckets place their fluid through the item's own use handler, not through the block-use path that minecraft:item_used_on_block fires from, so there is no attested trigger for it and I would have been inventing one. What ships checks the handover under fire, which is the whole joke anyway.
- **Going, Going, Gone** — THE ONE DETECTOR OF THIS BATCH, function-granted. Both criteria carry the diamonds_to_you distance-absolute-min-1 thrower guard, so neither can be armed by dropping and re-collecting your own Emeralds. 'bid' fires on a SELLER (somebody else picked his Emeralds up); bucket_fast opens a 600-tick window (bacaptmh_bid) on him and revokes the criterion in the same tick so it cannot re-fire every tick. 'won' fires on the BUYER; detect/going_going_gone revokes it first, then counts, with 'execute store result ... if entity' (never 'if entity @a[limit=3]'), how many non-spectators within eight blocks still have a live window, and grants to all of them plus @s at three or more. Three players are genuinely required and sufficient: A gives to B, B gives to C, C gives to A, and on that last pickup all three windows are live at once, so A trips the check with a count of three including himself. Two players can only ever reach a count of two. Honest limitations: the original called for strictly increasing piles and a single block, and neither survived - piles are not sized (the trigger reports the item type, not the stack that moved) and the floor is an eight-block radius rather than one block. What the pack sees is three people passing Emeralds around one spot inside thirty seconds.
- **Group Nap** — Sleeping is picked up two ways: the `slept` criterion for players who still need the advancement, and the vanilla sleep_in_bed statistic against a mirror for those who already hold it and can no longer latch criteria. Either one stamps a 60-second window on the player, and the grant fires when every non-spectator player online holds a live window at the same time. A real-time window replaces the old 'wipe the latch whenever daytime is under 1000' rule, so a frozen or externally driven clock cannot let people sleep sessions apart and still collect. The mirror is seeded at load and refreshed by an ungated bucket line, and it is not shared with any other advancement.
- **Hot Potato** — The check is driven from a middle link - a player who both received an item from someone and had an item of theirs picked up by someone inside the same 10 seconds, which is what being in the middle of a relay produces and which the min-distance-1 guard stops you from faking with your own item. The chain around that link is givers, plus receivers who did not give, plus players who already hold the advancement and whose Items Dropped statistic rose inside the same 10 seconds - the only live channel a holder still has, since a granted advancement stops latching criteria. Simply being present is not enough for anybody. It counts hand-offs, not item identity, so it cannot prove the same potato made the whole trip. All stamps are zeroed on the grant.
- **Lost and Found** — Two pure criteria ANDed. 'went_down' is minecraft:entity_killed_player with no conditions, the trigger BACAP uses in adventure/rekt.json - it fires only on a real death caused by an entity, so falling in a hole does not count. 'brought_back' is thrown_item_picked_up_by_player on a Recovery Compass whose thrower is a player at distance at least 1; the thrower of your own drop is measured against your own position and is always at distance 0, so the guard self-excludes exactly as mining/diamonds_to_you does. The original idea wanted a full inventory snapshot reconciled through a third party inside 2400 ticks and was dropped as a function-layer state machine; what survives is the part the game can actually see. The two criteria latch independently and are not time-linked - a death last week and a compass handed over today still complete it, the same looseness BACAP accepts throughout.
- **Market Day** — Pure criteria, no function. The four trade criteria use the VillagerData nbt string verbatim from BACAP's Master Trader, minus its level:5 clause, so any tier of Farmer, Butcher, Cartographer and Mason counts; NBT matching is subset matching, so leaving type and level out is correct rather than sloppy. 'funded' is the Emerald hand-over with the distance-min-1 guard and is the only half that cannot be done alone. DELIBERATE CHANGE: the original wanted four DIFFERENT players trading four different careers inside one village in a minute. Pure criteria can prove that another player was involved but never that four distinct players were - every criterion is evaluated against one player's own history - so players drops from four to two and the village-radius and one-minute clauses are gone with the detector. What survives is the shape of a market day: four stalls worked in one trip on a partner's money.
- **Matching Outfits** — Every 5 s each player's four armour slots are read live with `execute if items`, matching the leather item in each slot together with an exact minecraft:dyed_color value, so the check really is leather armour and not merely something carrying that component - the omission BACAP never makes in animal/fashion_statement. The matched colour index is written to a score and any two players inside 16 blocks sharing it are granted; the proximity is what makes it an outfit rather than two strangers in two dimensions. Because nothing lives in advancement criteria, a player who already holds it still counts as somebody's matching partner. Only the sixteen exact vanilla dye values are recognised, not custom mixed shades.
- **Orchestra** — A horn blast is detected from the vanilla used:goat_horn statistic against a per-cycle mirror rather than from advancement criteria, which is what lets a player who already owns the advancement still play their part; the mirror is seeded at load and refreshed by an ungated bucket line, so it can never read a lifetime total as a fresh blast, and it is not shared with the jump or sleep mirrors. The blower's held horn is inspected with `execute if items` to find which of the eight instruments sounded, that instrument's flag is raised, the player is tagged and the window is opened. The window is fixed, not rolling: only the first blast of a chain sets the five-second timer and later blasts do not extend it, so four instruments really do have to sound inside five seconds of the first one rather than inside a chain of blasts spaced under five seconds apart. It grants when four different instrument flags and four different tagged players are live at once, so one player blowing four horns is not an orchestra. Every flag, the timer and the tags are cleared on the grant and when the window lapses.
- **Pay It Forward** — Two vanilla criteria ANDed. 'handed_over' is thrown_item_picked_up_by_player on a Totem of Undying whose thrower is a player at distance at least 1 — the thrower of your own drop is always at distance 0 from you, so this is self-excluding exactly as fair_trade's 'got' is. 'burned_it' is used_totem, which only ever fires on a blow that would have killed you, so the spare's 'below two hearts' clause is enforced by the game itself rather than by a health read. No detector. The two criteria latch independently and are not time-linked: a totem taken off a teammate in one session and a different totem burned a week later still completes it, which is the same looseness BACAP accepts everywhere.
- **Pocket Money** — The item predicate on the thrown stack carries a minecraft:bundle_contents sub-predicate whose contains list is copied straight from BACAP's animal/flamboyant_range, so all four supplies must be inside the same bundle at the moment the other player picks it up. The original 'four different items' wording became four named items with counts on purpose: contains is attested in BACAP and Enhanced Discoveries, whereas the CollectionPredicate size field appears in neither, and an unattested field is exactly what I am not willing to guess at. Eight of each is 32 of the bundle's 64 capacity units, so there is room to spare and no risk of the kit not fitting.
- **Pony Express** — Pure criteria, no function. Both halves are Written Book handovers with the distance-min-1 thrower guard, and each carries a player predicate pinning the dimension - the entity_properties + location.dimension shape BACAP uses in challenges/root and end/the_actual_end. DELIBERATE CHANGE: the thousand-block relay through three carriers is not expressible without a function that tags an item stack and accumulates distance, so distance is replaced by a dimension crossing, which no single handover can fake and which is a real journey through a portal. Consequently players drops from three to two: pure criteria can prove that SOMEBODY else was involved, never that two different somebodies were, so a three-carrier chain cannot be distinguished from one partner used twice. Tier lowered from challenge to goal to match what is actually being asked.
- **Raid Party** — Every 5 s the detector reads Hero of the Village straight off every non-spectator player's active_effects and grants when three carry it at once. Reading NBT rather than a minecraft:location criterion removes the phase dependence: location fires on each player's own 20-tick cycle, and tagging from it while revoking in the same pass made the count depend on whose cycle happened to align with the sampling pass.
- **Reunion** — Every 5 s each player's isolation streak advances if no other non-spectator player is within 5000 blocks and is wiped to zero the instant one is. Ten minutes of streak (120 cycles) earns a permanent ticket; two ticket holders standing within 3 blocks are reunited. The streak, not a sticky tag, is what makes it honest: hopping through a Nether portal and walking back only earns a ticket if you actually stay away for ten minutes, so the fact that a distance selector cannot see across dimensions no longer buys anything. The ticket is never taken back, so a veteran can be the other half of a newcomer's reunion, and the streak resets at 120 so nothing grows without bound.
- **Secret Santa** — The same self-pickup-guarded give/get pair as Fair Trade on a 10-second timer, so dropping and re-collecting your own item latches nothing. A player who still needs the advancement counts as a participant only by holding both live timers - a real hand-off out and a real hand-off in. A player who already holds it can never latch a criterion again, so they count from a live channel that survives the grant instead: the vanilla Items Dropped statistic, stamped with its own 10-second window by an ungated bucket line whenever it rises. That is deliberately weaker evidence than gave+got, and it is why the description says players swapping items rather than claiming every one of the four is verified to have both given and received; what it buys is that a veteran must actually be throwing items into the pile to make up the numbers for a newcomer, instead of merely standing nearby, and that a player who joins the server late is not locked out forever. Four participants inside 16 blocks grants to the live traders; the timers are reset on the grant.
- **Split the Party** — Every 5 s the detector walks every non-spectator player, and the first player found in each dimension raises that dimension's flag and is tagged. When all three flags are up it grants to the three tagged players only, so a crowd standing next to whoever holds the Overworld slot gets nothing. Needs three players online, counted with `store result ... if entity`, or it returns after two commands.
- **Starter Kit** — Three thrown_item_picked_up_by_player criteria latch when another player's dropped sword, pickaxe and food are collected; entity is entity_type minecraft:player with distance.absolute.min 1, and for this trigger 'entity' is the THROWER, so a self-drop puts the thrower at distance 0 and is excluded outright. Vanilla latches criteria forever, so detect/starter_kit revokes all three every medium pass unless bacaptmh_dtime (the death stamp detect/sample maintains ungated for every non-spectator) is newer than gametime minus 600 — that revocation, not the criteria, is what enforces 'died in the last thirty seconds'. Requirements are four AND-ed groups including the impossible guard, so criteria alone can never complete it; the detector grants to the resupplied player and everyone within 12 blocks, because the criteria carry no way to name the givers.
- **Team Photo** — Every 5 s, for each player who still lacks it, the detector counts non-spectator players within 3 blocks (self included) and grants to all of them when the count reaches 4. The count is a real `store result ... if entity` count, not a `limit=4` selector test, and it includes players who already hold the advancement, so veterans can still make up the numbers for a newcomer.
- **The Wave** — A minecraft.custom:minecraft.jump objective is compared against a previous-tick mirror, so a jump is seen on the tick it happens. The mirror is refreshed by an ungated line in bucket_fast that runs every tick whether or not the detector did, and it is seeded at load, so it can never freeze while fewer than four players are online and then read a backlog of jumps as four simultaneous ones the moment a fourth player joins. Each new jumper who is not already in the chain is tagged, bumps the chain counter and refreshes a 10-tick window; four tagged players grant to all of them and the chain resets. The window is rolling, which is why the description says each jump is within half a second of the last rather than claiming all four fall inside one fixed half second. Spectators and creative players are excluded.
- **Trust Fall** — A minecraft.used:minecraft.water_bucket delta opens a sixty-second window on whoever pours; only while some window is open does the fast bucket track anyone at all, and then only players who are not on the ground, so the per-tick FallDistance read is bounded. The running peak per player is kept in bacaptmh_fall. Landing in a minecraft:water block with a peak of 30+, with a windowed pourer other than the faller within 24 blocks, grants both and zeroes the peak. The bank-a-dive exploit is closed: the peak is cleared by ANY water, not just by touching ground, so diving 30 blocks into a natural lake and staying in it no longer leaves a live 30 sitting there waiting for someone to place a bucket nearby. Tracking is no longer gated on the faller lacking the advancement, so a veteran can still act as the faller for a pourer who needs it. Spectators and creative are excluded on both ends.
- **Twin Maps** — Pure criteria, no detector. `handed_a_copy` is thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity with item filled_map and the picker pinned to entity_type player plus the distance absolute min 1 self-exclusion from mining/diamonds_to_you, so it only fires when a DIFFERENT player picks up a Filled Map you dropped; `kept_one` is inventory_changed showing you still hold a Filled Map. requirements AND the two groups, so one alone never completes it. Honest limitation: vanilla item predicates expose no map_id, so the pack verifies 'you gave a player a map and still have one', not that the two stacks are literally copies of the same map — the original 'both markers showing' wording was dropped for that reason.
- **Vault Crew** — The `unlock` criterion is BACAP's own Revaulting condition - an ominous trial key used on a vault whose ominous state is true. It is now only a trigger, not the whole count. When it fires, a short twelve-step line-of-sight walk from the player's eyes finds the ominous vault they just used the key on and reads `server_data.rewarded_players` off the block: a vault keeps a list of every player it has already rewarded, so 'the same vault, four different people' is read from the world instead of guessed. Reaching four grants. A second, independent path covers a crew that opens one vault together in one go: four live unlockers inside 8 blocks and 10 seconds. Neither path counts a player merely because they already hold the advancement and happen to be standing there - the previous version did, and one unlocker beside three idle veterans was enough to finish it. Stamps are zeroed on the grant.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Caravan of Love** — Reframed from the original leash-chain idea, which is not detectable: a llama caravan lives in the LlamaEntity caravanHead field and is never written to NBT (that is why caravans break on world reload), and BACAP's own 'You Lead, I'll Follow' checks nothing more than a lead used on a llama. So this ANDs that exact BACAP criterion (player_interacted_with_entity with a lead on #blazeandcave:llamas) with taking a Lead off another player's drop, using the same distance-min-1 thrower guard. What the pack can actually see is 'another player supplied the lead you hitched a llama with'; it cannot see whose caravan the llama joined, and the description is written to claim only slightly more than that, in line with the BACAP advancement it hangs off.
- **Carpool** — For each player still missing it, the detector walks 'execute on vehicle ... on passengers' and counts how many of that vehicle's passengers are non-spectator players; if the vehicle is in #blazeandcave:all_boats and the count is two or more, every player aboard is granted. A pure started_riding criterion cannot express this because its vehicle.passenger predicate also matches the mounting player themselves, and players are never written into a vehicle's Passengers NBT, so the passenger walk is the only reliable count. Per-rider counting is correct here because the requirement is two riders in the same boat.
- **Cavalry Charge** — Run at a player who lacks it: every non-spectator within 15 blocks is stepped onto their vehicle, and each vehicle that is a minecraft:horse inside 15 blocks is tagged. The count is then of TAGGED HORSES, not of riders, so the threshold cannot be met by stacking riders on one animal even if a future mount gains extra seats; at four or more distinct mounted horses the passengers of those horses are granted. The tag is cleared before and after the count, and both clears are radius-limited to the same 15 blocks, which is exact because no time passes inside one function execution.
- **Good Boy, Someone Else's** — Pure criteria. 'patched_him_up' is copied verbatim from the vanilla advancement minecraft:husbandry/repair_wolf_armor as BACAP ships it - player_interacted_with_entity, item armadillo_scute, entity a wolf whose body equipment is wolf_armor with minecraft:damage 0 - so it is guaranteed to behave exactly as 'Good as New' does. 'handed_the_scutes' is the distance-min-1 thrower guard on a scute pickup. Honest limitation, and the reason the title moved from the dog to the scutes: an entity predicate has no owner field and there is no non-macro way to compare a wolf's Owner UUID to the acting player, so the pack cannot see whose dog it is. What it can see is that the scutes came out of another player's hands, which is what the description now claims.
- **Guard Dog** — Pure criteria. 'befriended' is BACAP's own Leader of the Pack criterion, character for character - minecraft:tame_animal with entity_type minecraft:wolf. 'handed_the_bones' is the standard distance-min-1 thrower guard on a Bone pickup. Substitution stated plainly: the original wanted a mob killed by a wolf whose owner is a different player while that mob was targeting you, and no vanilla trigger fires on a player for a kill their pet made - player_killed_entity requires the player to land the blow - so the borrowed-pet act is not observable at all. Taming the dog on somebody else's bones is the nearest thing the criteria layer can actually witness, and it keeps the tab, the tier and the two-player requirement.
- **Hog Wild** — minecraft:location is a periodic trigger — vanilla fires it for every player every 20 ticks — so it works as a free once-a-second state check with no detection function at all. The check is: my vehicle is a pig, and the entity my crosshair raycast lands on is a player whose own vehicle is a pig, no more than 5 blocks away. looking_at cannot resolve to yourself, so the second pig-rider is necessarily somebody else. A pig can only be mounted when saddled, so 'saddled' needs no separate test. Substitution note: the original asked for 100 blocks travelled side by side; distance-travelled state cannot be held in criteria, so the advancement asks for the formation instead of the mileage — riding abreast and glancing over is exactly the pose it was describing.
- **Horse Trading** — Both ends of the handover carry a vehicle predicate of entity_type minecraft:horse — the receiver through player, the thrower through entity — using the same vehicle field BACAP reads in monsters/absolute_cinema, so the criterion only fires when two mounted riders are involved. Taken from spares in place of Fifty-Fifty and reframed: the original wanted each rider on the horse the OTHER tamed, which needs the horse's Owner UUID compared against a player at runtime and so needs a function. Riders on adjacent horses sit comfortably past the distance min 1 self-exclusion guard; if they are squeezed closer than that the criterion simply waits until they step apart.
- **Pack Leaders** — Two radii, both stated: wolves are searched and tagged within 24 blocks of the running player, and the qualifying players are the non-spectators within 10 blocks of them. Wolves within 24 blocks are tested with 'if items entity @s armor.body minecraft:wolf_armor' and tagged bacaptmh_armored. Each candidate player then CLAIMS one armoured wolf within 8 blocks that no one else has claimed: if such a wolf exists the player is tagged bacaptmh_leader and the nearest free wolf is tagged bacaptmh_claimed, so one shared wolf can satisfy exactly one player. Three leaders are therefore three players each with their own armoured wolf; the separate 'three wolves in the area' count is gone because the claim already implies it. Wolf Armor can only be equipped on a tamed wolf, so the armour test doubles as the tamed test; ownership is inferred from the wolf sticking near its player rather than from the Owner UUID, which commands cannot compare. All three tags are cleared at both ends of the function.
- **Passing the Lead** — 'handed' fires when you pick up a Lead that another player threw (thrown_item_picked_up_by_player, item lead, thrower predicate entity_type player + distance min 1, so your own dropped lead cannot satisfy it). 'leashed' is minecraft:player_interacted_with_entity with item lead, exactly the trigger BACAP animal/follow_the_leader uses for attaching a lead to a mob. Requirements AND the two. The original idea wanted the SECOND player to leash the SAME animal the first was leading; proving mob identity needs a detector and the budget was spent elsewhere, so what is checked is 'his lead, your leash'.
- **Photo Finish** — Detector. bucket_medium computes, for every non-spectator every 10-tick window, the delta of minecraft.custom:minecraft.horse_one_cm against a private mirror seeded in seed_lines. 500 cm in ten ticks is five blocks in half a second, i.e. ten blocks a second, which only a fast horse sustains. detect/photo_finish then runs as/at each survival rider over that threshold and grants to everyone within three blocks who is also over it; distance=0.5..3 excludes @s himself, because an entity measured from its own position is at distance exactly 0. Caveat stated plainly: horse_one_cm covers horses, donkeys and mules but not camels or striders, so this is a horseback advancement.
- **Pit Crew** — minecraft:player_interacted_with_entity with a horse-food item and an entity predicate whose `passenger` is a player - the same nested-passenger shape BACAP uses in monsters/lancelot and nether/giddy_up. The rider is required to be at least one block from the feeder, and nested predicates are matched against the same context origin (the feeder's position), so feeding the horse you are yourself riding scores 0 and is excluded. 'Back to full health' is dropped - entity health is not expressible in an entity predicate and a detector was not worth a task.
- **Release the Hounds** — DETECTOR 1 of 5. Vanilla gives the kill credit for a pet kill to the wolf, not its owner, so the only trigger that fires is entity_killed_player on the VICTIM (criterion `mauled`, matching killer entity_type wolf); the impossible guard is ANDed in a separate requirements group so that flag alone can never complete the advancement. bucket_fast runs detect/release_the_hounds as the flagged victim `at @s` — the datapack tick tag runs at the end of the same server tick the death was processed in, before any respawn packet, so @s is still standing on the death spot. It revokes the flag (consuming it), tags itself bacaptmh_mauled, then walks @e[type=wolf,distance=..24,limit=12,sort=nearest] `on owner` and grants to every owner that is a non-spectator player and is not the victim. Known weakness: the wolf that landed the blow is not identified, so a wild-wolf kill next to somebody else's tamed wolf would credit that owner; and `execute on owner` is not attested anywhere in the installed packs, only in vanilla command grammar.
- **Reservoir Dogs** — Function-granted by detect/reservoir_dogs, dispatched every medium tick as each non-spectator missing it who has any Wolf within 24 blocks. Wolves linger at Health 0.0f through a ~20-tick death animation, so a 10-tick sampler always catches at least two frames of it. The detector examines exactly ONE dying wolf per pass (limit=1,sort=nearest), so the dead wolf's owner and the killer's owner can never be read out of two different fights: 'execute as <dying wolf> on owner' tags the loser, and 'on attacker if entity @s[type=wolf] on owner' tags the winner - the vanilla attacker relation resolves to the entity that last damaged the wolf, so a wolf killed by a player, a mob or fall damage yields no winner tag at all. The grant needs a loser tag to exist and goes only to a winner-tagged player who is not also loser-tagged, which is what enforces 'two different owners, both online players'. Deliberately one-sided: the losing owner is not granted, since the advancement says win. Caveat I am not fully certain of: the attacker relation reflects the last thing that hurt the wolf, so a wolf softened by another player's wolf and finished by something else would not count, and a wolf finished by another player's wolf after being softened by anything else would.
- **Shared Custody** — Naming an animal stamps the namer's integer player id onto that animal in the bacaptmh_owner objective. Feeding an animal whose stamp is somebody else's id grants to the feeder and to the player whose id it is. The comparison is integer-only, so unlike the name-matching version it never puts player-controlled text near a command.
- **Sting Operation** — Two criteria that can never both belong to the same player: 'angered' fires for whoever hits a bee (player_hurt_entity, the same shape as BACAP's mob-hurt entries, with the distance min 1 guard), 'stung' fires for whoever a bee kills (entity_killed_player with entity_type bee, exactly the shape BACAP's Endergeddon uses). detect/sting_operation stamps the angerer with a 30-second bacaptmh_stt window and releases their flag so the next bee re-arms it; when a stung player is processed it grants to every OTHER non-spectator within 64 blocks still inside that window, then to the victim, and only releases the stung flag if nobody was in range. The distance=1..64 selector excludes the victim themselves, so a lone player who angers their own hive and dies to it gets nothing. Both grants happen before the stung flag is released, so a completed advancement is never un-completed by the revoke.
- **Two-Hump Transit** — Identical passenger walk to Carpool but the vehicle must be a minecraft:camel; when one camel carries two non-spectator player passengers both riders are granted.

</details>

| ◆ | **Thar She Blows** | Biomes | Kill a Drowned with a thrown Trident from a boat another player is sharing with you (2 players) | 50 |
| ◆ | **The Cousteau Manoeuvre** | Biomes | Ride Nautiluses forty blocks through an Ocean Monument with another player, neither surfacing nor dismounting (2 players) | 220 |
| · | **The Merchant of Venice** | Biomes | Complete an item swap with another player while you are both sitting in boats on water (2 players) | 30 |

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Cold Chain** — The receiver's biome is read through player.location.biomes, the plural field name BACAP uses in enchanting/let_it_go, and the supplier is pinned to a player at distance min 1 so nobody can hand the bucket to himself. Taken from spares in place of Trickle Down, which needed a per-giver tally of distinct receiver UUIDs and therefore a function. The haul is enforced by the game rather than by the pack: powder snow only exists in freezing biomes, so the bucket physically has to be carried to the desert, and the criterion fires on arrival.
- **Cold Shoulder** — Pure criteria, no detector. One minecraft:player_killed_entity criterion whose victim predicate is entity_type player (so it can only be another player — you cannot be your own killed entity, which is why no distance guard is needed here), nbt {TicksFrozen:140} and location.block powder_snow. TicksFrozen is clamped at exactly 140 by the freezing tick, and BACAP's nether/polar_opposites already matches it with the identical literal-NBT form; location.block on an entity predicate is the same LocationPredicate that blazeandcave:monsters/freezing uses for biomes and that bacaped:mining/the_master_of_falls uses with powder_snow. Documented reframe: the original idea wanted the victim to die of freeze damage, but a freeze death has no source entity so no vanilla trigger attributes it to the player who built the trap; killing the fully-frozen victim yourself is the closest pure-criteria expression.
- **Conduit Club** — A single NBT pass tags every non-spectator holding minecraft:conduit_power, then anchors on each tagged player and counts tagged players within 48 blocks. A full-power conduit projects 96 blocks, so a 48-block cluster cannot straddle two conduits in two different oceans — the old global @a count let three players at three separate conduits qualify. Three in range grants exactly those three; the tag is cleared at both ends of the function so nothing leaks.
- **Crow's Nest** — One using_item criterion, modelled on BACAP adventure/hey_you_two_should_kiss (same trigger, same spyglass item predicate). vehicle pins a #minecraft:boat (the entity-type tag BACAP already uses) and its passenger sub-predicate — the field BACAP uses in monsters/lancelot and biomes/naughtylus — pins a second player. The distance guard is 0.3 rather than the usual 1 because a boat's two seats sit roughly 0.8 blocks apart: 0.3 still excludes the viewer himself (distance exactly 0) but admits the genuine second rider. #minecraft:is_deep_ocean is the biome tag BACAP uses in enchanting/let_it_go.
- **Ferryman's Fee** — Function-granted. Every medium tick detect/ferrymans_fee turns each candidate's boat_one_cm statistic into a delta against its own private mirror, then uses 'execute as @s on vehicle ... on passengers' to walk the real passenger list of the boat they are sitting in — so a second boat drawn up alongside is never counted as a passenger. The delta is only added to bacaptmh_ferry while two or more non-spectator, non-creative players share that boat, and the counter is zeroed the moment that stops being true, which is the dismount reset. boat_one_cm is awarded to passengers as well as the pilot, so both riders accumulate and both are granted at 200,000 cm. The delta is clamped to 1..2000 per pass, so even an unseeded mirror could not dump a lifetime boat total in one go. Dropped from the original idea: the 'open ocean, landmass to landmass' condition — a biome-path test is a second detector and the budget did not stretch to it, so the description promises only the distance.
- **Man Overboard** — fishing_rod_hooked fires on retrieval with `entity` bound to whatever the bobber caught — BACAP's Indiana Jones and Pig Fishing Tournament use exactly this shape for entities rather than fish. Here the catch must be a player who is in the swimming pose, at least a block away, while the angler sits in a boat (the #minecraft:boat entity tag, as used by BACAP's Hey! You two should kiss!) inside a #minecraft:is_deep_ocean biome (the tag BACAP uses in Let It Go!). Substitution note: 'more than 100 blocks from any land' is not expressible in a location predicate, so the deep-ocean biome stands in for open water. Caveat worth knowing: is_swimming is the sprint-swim pose, not merely being in water, so the castaway has to actually be swimming when the hook lands.
- **Sculk Silence** — Co-location is now required throughout, not just at the finish. Every five seconds each non-spectator standing in minecraft:deep_dark is tagged, and any Warden within 96 blocks strips the tag; a tagged player only ticks their counter up if at least three tagged players (themselves included) are within 48 blocks, otherwise it is reset to zero. Three people who each spent two quiet minutes in three separate pockets and then converged no longer qualify — the whole two minutes has to be spent as a group. At 24 ticks the group is granted and every granted counter is reset to zero, so a veteran standing around can never pad a later attempt with a frozen 24. Counting is deliberately not gated on lacking the advancement, so veterans can still make up the three for a newcomer. The streak is one of the counters f4_init clears, which is correct here: a /reload stops the ticking loop anyway, and this advancement is about two unbroken minutes.
- **Sea Legs** — Detection function. Every tick a player who lacks it and is not riding anything (`execute on vehicle` sets a flag, so a passenger gets Carpool rather than this) is tested for a boat hitbox 0.3 blocks under their feet with a different, non-spectator player among that boat's passengers. Distance comes from the driver's own boat_one_cm statistic: the surfer snapshots it when the run starts and adds each later delta, clamped to 0..2000 cm per tick so a boat swap or a stat rollover cannot jump the counter. Falling off resets the run and clears the snapshot; at 20000 cm both the surfer and every other player in the boat are granted.
- **Submarine Crew** — Same distinct-vehicle pattern as Cavalry Charge and Ghast Fleet: every non-spectator within 15 blocks is stepped onto their vehicle, each minecraft:nautilus so found is tagged, and the TAGGED NAUTILUSES are counted. Three distinct crewed nautiluses grants every player riding one of them. Counting vehicles rather than riders matters here because nothing in the reference material establishes a Nautilus's passenger capacity, so three players on one Nautilus must not satisfy 'three Nautiluses'.
- **Synchronised Swimming** — Function-granted. Every medium tick each eligible player's minecraft.custom:minecraft.swim_one_cm delta is compared against a private mirror; that statistic only accrues in the swimming pose, never from walking or treading water, so a positive delta over the 0.5 s window means 'was actually swimming'. Everyone with a positive delta is tagged bacaptmh_swimming - the tag is applied whether or not they already hold the advancement, so a holder can still make up the fourth swimmer. Each tagged player who lacks it then COUNTS the tagged players within 6 blocks with 'execute store result ... run execute if entity' (never 'if entity @a[limit=4]', which only tests for one match) and grants to all of them at four. The mirror is refreshed for every eligible player every window, ungated, and is seeded in seed_lines, so a lifetime swim distance can never be read as a fresh burst.
- **Thar She Blows** — The criterion copies BACAP poseidon_vs_hades exactly (is_projectile tag plus direct_entity trident) with entity drowned, and adds a player.vehicle predicate of #blazeandcave:all_boats so it can only latch while the killer is seated in a boat. It is paired with an impossible guard in a separate requirements group, so criteria alone never complete it. Every tick, detect/thar_she_blows runs for anyone holding the latched criterion, revokes it, then walks 'on vehicle on passengers' the way carpool.mcfunction does — players are never written into a boat's Passengers NBT, so the passenger walk is the only reliable count — and grants to every player aboard once the count reaches two. It cannot tell the pilot from the gunner; anyone in the boat when the kill lands is credited, which is the intended co-op payout.
- **The Cousteau Manoeuvre** — The criteria do the whole structure test: minecraft:location fires roughly every 20 ticks and the 'inside' criterion only arms while the player's vehicle is a minecraft:nautilus, the block at their feet is water or flowing_water, and location.structures resolves to minecraft:monument (the same LocationPredicate fields BACAP's Stayin' Frosty and Enhanced Discoveries' in_structure predicates already use). The medium bucket turns that flag into a four-window bacaptmh_cst stamp and releases the criterion so it re-arms; detect/cousteau then adds this window's nautilus_one_cm delta to bacaptmh_cous only while a second stamped non-spectator is within 16 blocks, and zeroes the tally the moment the player is alone. Surfacing or dismounting stops the criterion arming, cst decays to 0 and a separate bucket line wipes the tally, so the forty blocks (4000 cm) have to be continuous and paired. The nautilus mirror is seeded in seed_lines and its delta is consumed ungated every medium window, so a lifetime ride total can never be read as one window's travel.
- **The Merchant of Venice** — A real two-way swap: 'gave' is thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity (somebody else picked up what you threw) and 'got' is thrown_item_picked_up_by_player (you picked up what somebody else threw); both are required, so one-way charity does not count. Each side demands that the other party is a player at distance min 1 riding a #blazeandcave:all_boats entity, and that you are in one too. 'On water' is an any_of over the boat's stepping_on block being water and the boat's own location fluid being water - one of the two holds for a floating boat, and the pair hedges which of the two vanilla actually reports for a boat resting on the surface.
- **Warm Welcome** — One minecraft:location criterion, which vanilla fires for every player every 20 ticks, so this is a free once-a-second state check with no function. The check is: Flint and Steel in my main hand (the equipment.mainhand field BACAP uses in monsters/captain_etho), and my crosshair raycast lands on a player no more than 5 blocks away whose own feet block is minecraft:powder_snow (looking_at as in BACAP adventure/hey_you_two_should_kiss, location.block.blocks as in BACAP enchanting/silent_but_deadly). looking_at can never resolve to yourself, so the buried player is necessarily somebody else and the whole thing is impossible alone. Stated openly, because an earlier pass dropped this idea for exactly this reason: vanilla exposes no frozen state to predicates - TicksFrozen is NBT-only and an nbt predicate cannot express a range - so the advancement asks for the rescue pose rather than the thaw. It fires the moment you stand over them with the fire-starter out, not when their freeze meter drains, and the description was rewritten to claim only that.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Barn Raising** — requirements are now [["place"],["impossible"]] — two AND-ed groups that between them name every criterion, so AdvancementRequirements.validate is satisfied and the file loads, while the impossible group still means placing blocks can never complete it by itself. The fast bucket selects on advancements={bacaptmh:building/barn_raising={place=true,impossible=false}}, so a player who already holds it (all criteria true) is never re-entered and the revoke can never un-complete a finished advancement. The chunk key is fixed three ways: the coordinate is read raw and floor-divided by 16 through a scoreboard /= (which is floorDiv, unlike the old 0.0625 scale that truncated toward zero and made a 32-block bucket around the origin), a dimension score joins x and z in the key, and the tally is reset to zero for every player granted so a veteran can never pad a later group with a frozen 100. The tally is also the one persistent counter in f4_init, so it is created there with 'add 0' rather than cleared with 'set 0': start_extra deliberately clears bacaptmh_init on every /reload to reseed the statistic mirrors, and the old 'set 0' meant every reload wiped three builders' in-progress block counts at once. bacaptmh_cx/cz/cd were already left untouched, so the chunk key survives the reload and still matches the tally. Granting uses a plain 'advancement grant @s only', which sets both criteria. Known limit: a player who already holds this cannot help a newcomer, because a completed advancement stops listening to its own criteria — the pack has no parentless technical advancement to hang the flag on, since every entry here is forced to carry a parent, a display and a milestone slot.
- **Bricked In** — The 'place' criterion only arms the check - the impossible guard in its own requirement group keeps the advancement from ever completing on criteria alone. Every tick, for a player who has placed a block, detect/bricked_in looks at each other survival-mode player within 8 blocks and tests the six faces of their feet block (floor, ceiling at head+1, and the four sides) against air, cave_air, void_air, water and lava by literal id - no block tag is used, because an unknown tag would be a parse error and would take the whole function file with it. Both the bricklayer and the entombed player are granted. It does not verify that the walls are new or that the victim never moved.
- **Bunk Beds** — Function-granted, entirely from bucket_medium lines - no detect file of its own. A private mirror of minecraft.custom:minecraft.sleep_in_bed opens a 20-window (10 s) bacaptmh_bunk window when a player gets into a bed. A player with an open window who is standing in a bed block ('if block ~ ~ ~ #minecraft:beds' - a sleeping player is positioned inside the bed block) then looks for a second open-window player in the same 1x1 block column, using 'positioned ~-0.5 ~1.9 ~-0.5' with dx=1,dy=8,dz=1. A sleeping player's hitbox is 0.2 x 0.2, so that box is exact: a bed two blocks up qualifies and a bed one block up does not. The mirrored downward test ('positioned ~-0.5 ~-9.9 ~-0.5') runs for every candidate too, so each sleeper is granted from their own iteration and no cross-player identity bookkeeping is needed. Honest limitation: the partner is only proven to be a recent sleeper standing in the same column, not proven to be lying in a bed at that exact instant, because a selector cannot test another entity's block.
- **Campfire Stories** — The sneak flag is a pure criterion: minecraft:location re-evaluates every 20 ticks for every player (BACAP enchanting/silent_but_deadly uses this trigger for a live is_sneaking check), so it latches while a player is crouched. requirements are [[sneak],[impossible]], an AND of groups, so the flag alone can never complete the advancement. bucket_medium decays bacaptmh_cs, stamps it to 3 for anyone carrying the flag, then revokes the flag, giving a 1.5 s 'crouched recently' window that survives the trigger's 20-tick cadence. Night is gametime modulo 24000 inside 13000..22999; time query daytime does not parse here, so this is the available approximation and it drifts from the real time of day if anybody uses /time set. detect/campfire then runs at each crouched candidate, scores 1 if any of the 18 blocks in the 3x3 column at foot level and one below is a lit #minecraft:campfires block, and otherwise returns. It counts crouched non-spectator, non-creative players within 5 blocks with a real head count rather than a limit= selector, and grants to all of them at four.
- **Community Beacon** — Beacons apply their effects with ambient set, which drunk or splashed potions never are — the same discriminator BACAP itself uses in nether/beacon_overlord. Six tag passes mark every non-spectator carrying an ambient Speed, Haste, Resistance, Jump Boost, Strength or Regeneration; each tagged player is then an anchor and four tagged players within 24 blocks grants all of them. The radius came down from 50 to 24 specifically because 50 is a full-power beacon's own reach, so two beacons up to 100 blocks apart could pool their crowds; 24 blocks cannot span two beacons' effect volumes in any useful way. The beacon block itself is still not located — a block scan over a 100x400x100 box every five seconds is not affordable — so the guarantee is 'four beacon-buffed players standing together', which is what the advancement is actually about.
- **Firework Finale** — A minecraft.used:minecraft.firework_rocket delta opens a six-medium-tick window, which is exactly three seconds rather than the 3.5 the old value gave. The check now anchors on a launcher and counts windowed non-spectators within 32 blocks, so four people setting off rockets in three different dimensions no longer share a finale. Rockets burnt for Elytra thrust still count as launches.
- **Gallery Opening** — Paintings are entities, so placed_block never fires for them; item_used_on_block does, because ItemStack.useOn fires that trigger for any successful use of an item against a block — which is how Enhanced Discoveries' 'Art Lover' counts painting placements. The item is matched with match_tool inside the location list, the only place item_used_on_block exposes the stack. The criterion latches, an impossible guard in its own requirements group stops it completing on its own, and detect/gallery_opening (medium bucket, gamemode=!spectator and !creative) revokes it, stamps a 120-medium-tick (one minute) window on that player, counts players inside 12 blocks whose window is still open, and grants to all of them at four. The detector is deliberately NOT gated on the online player count, so a latched criterion is always consumed on the next pass and cannot sit around waiting to pair with a painting hung days later. What is verified is four distinct players hanging paintings within 12 blocks inside a minute; painting variants are not compared, so four copies of the same painting count.
- **Guest Room** — 'gift_bed' fires when you pick up a bed another player threw (#minecraft:beds is attested in BACAP; distance min 1 excludes picking your own back up) and 'slept' is the bare slept_in_bed trigger Enhanced Discoveries uses in Good Dreams!. Requirements AND the two, and the gift half is unsatisfiable alone. Moved from the Adventure tab to Building because every bed-related parent in BACAP (Change of Sheets, Rainbow Dreams, Insomniac) lives in Building and the Adventure tab has none. The 'dyed, not white' clause was dropped: the bed's colour would have to be read from the block under the sleeping player, which is not a shape either reference pack demonstrates.
- **Library Card** — 'stocked' is BACAP building/shelf_expression.json with its six slot_N_occupied state constraints removed - same trigger, same location_check plus match_tool pair, same four book ids - so one book into a Chiseled Bookshelf is enough. 'lent' is the outward pickup trigger (thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity, the diamonds_to_you shape) with entity_type player and the distance min 1 guard on the borrower, which self-excludes at distance 0. The spare wanted the taker matched against the stocker at a stored block position, which is a detector plus per-position state; reversing the direction - you are the librarian, you shelve and you lend - gets the same two-player act out of pure criteria. The criteria do not link the lent book to the shelved one.
- **Required Reading** — Two criteria ANDed in separate requirement groups: taking a signed written_book off another player's drop, and using a written_book on a lectern block, the latter written with the location_check plus match_tool pair BACAP uses in adventure/im_not_lost_anymore rather than a bare item field. The original framing wanted the book's author component compared against the placing player, which criteria cannot do — a component predicate can only match a fixed value, never 'not you' — so authorship is carried by the handover instead. The two legs are not time-linked and the pack cannot prove the book on the lectern is the same one you were given; the description claims only what is checked.
- **Trading Post** — Three vanilla criteria, ANDed. 'stocked' copies BACAP's Shelfception line for line - item_used_on_block with a location_check on the #minecraft:wooden_shelves block tag - which is the working proof that this trigger does fire when an item is put onto a Shelf. 'priced' is placed_block on #minecraft:all_signs, the block tag BACAP already uses. 'paid' is thrown_item_picked_up_by_player on an emerald with the thrower at distance.absolute.min 1, so the emerald must have left somebody else's hand. The till is not modelled - there is no trigger for taking an item out of a container - so the description was reworded from 'out of the till' to being paid by the customer, which is also the half that makes it impossible alone.
- **Trophy Wall** — Pure criteria, no detector. `mounted` copies BACAP adventure/map_room's structure exactly — player_interacted_with_entity with an item predicate and an any_of over item_frame / glow_item_frame — swapping filled_map for player_head. `handed_the_head` is the same thrown_item_picked_up_by_player handover used across this batch, so the head must come from another player's hands. Honest limitation: no vanilla item predicate exposes the profile component, so 'that player's OWN head' and 'while they watch' are not verified; what is verified is that another player put a player head in your hands and you framed one. In survival a player head only exists because a Charged Creeper killed a player, which is itself impossible alone.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Book Club** — Pure criteria, no detector. minecraft:enchanted_item reports the RESULT of the enchant, so a book enchanted at a table matches minecraft:enchanted_book, not minecraft:book. 'shared' is minecraft:thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity, which fires for the player who THREW the item with entity set to whoever collected it - BACAP mining/diamonds_to_you is the reference, including the distance min 1 guard, which here also excludes the thrower picking his own book back up. Requirements default to AND. Reworded from 'two players enchant at the same table within ten seconds', which needed a detector to pair two enchanters at one table - see report.
- **Death and Taxes** — DETECTOR 5 of 5, function-granted (impossible criterion). Vanilla has no experience-pickup trigger, so this is two ungated mirrors plus one gated check. In bucket_fast a private deathCount objective (bacaptmh_dcount, consumed and zeroed on read so a lifetime total can never leak) fires detect/tax_death `at @s` in the same tick the player dies, before any respawn, stamping the death block into bacaptmh_tx/ty/tz and opening a 300-tick window. In bucket_medium every player's experience points are turned into a delta with a single `execute store result score @s bacaptmh_xpd run experience query @s points` followed by two scoreboard operations — maintained UNGATED for @a, so the mirror cannot go stale behind the advancement gate. Any non-spectator, non-creative player who lacks the advancement and gained points that window runs detect/tax_claim, which grants both the collector and the corpse's owner if the collector stands within 8 blocks on each axis of a death spot whose window is still open. Honest limitations: XP gained from a furnace or a bottle within eight blocks of a fresh corpse would also count, the sampling window is 10 ticks so a very fast pickup-and-run is possible to miss, and with keepInventory on no experience drops at all.
- **Grant Money** — Function-granted. Every 10 ticks detect/grant_money runs for each player without the advancement and reads the delta of his minecraft.used:minecraft.experience_bottle statistic against a mirror seeded in bacaptmh:seed. Any player within 6 blocks of the thrower is credited with that many bottles (his XpLevel is snapshotted the first time, straight from NBT rather than from a scoreboard criterion, so no new objective criterion can fail to parse) and the thrower's donor window is refreshed for 600 ticks. At 64 credited bottles, if the beneficiary's level is above his snapshot, both he and every donor within 16 blocks are granted. The original '+30 levels' target was dropped: a stack of bottles is roughly 450 XP, which is nowhere near 30 levels from any starting point, so the check is simply that the levels landed on him.
- **Hand-Me-Down** — Two AND groups. Group one: 'handed' — thrown_item_picked_up_by_player where the picked-up item carries minecraft:binding_curse and the thrower is another player at distance min 1. Group two is an OR over the four armour slots, each an inventory_changed criterion copied verbatim from BACAP enchanting/handmade_blinding's equipment/predicates/enchantments shape (equipping armour changes the inventory, so the trigger fires). requirements is AND-of-groups, never one group containing both. No function.
- **Inheritance** — 'thirty_levels' copies BACAP statistics/giga_xp_grinder.json verbatim apart from the number: a minecraft:location trigger reading minecraft:type_specific/player level, which the location trigger re-evaluates every 20 ticks. 'handed_down' is the diamonds_to_you thrower guard applied to Bottles o' Enchanting - entity_type player with distance min 1, which self-excludes because your own throw is at distance 0 from you. The original wanted experience orbs tagged with a dead player's UUID, which nothing in criteria can see and which no amount of function would make reliable; the replacement keeps the shape of the idea - somebody else's experience becoming yours - through the one XP carrier the game lets a player physically hand over. The two criteria latch independently, so the levels do not have to come from those specific bottles; the description says 'off', not 'entirely from'.
- **Lapis Loan** — Pure criteria, no detector. 'lapis' copies BACAP adventure/undying_fandom's lapis pickup criterion but swaps the villager thrower for entity_type player plus distance min 1, so only lapis another player threw you counts. 'enchanted' is minecraft:enchanted_item, the trigger blazeandcave:enchanting/root itself uses. Both are ANDed. Honest limitation: the 1200-tick window in the idea is dropped because enforcing it would need a detector, so the enchant does not have to follow the handover immediately — only both must happen.
- **Parting Gift** — Pure criteria, no detector. `cursed_gift` fires when you pick up an item another player threw whose enchantments predicate contains minecraft:vanishing_curse (the item-predicate shape is BACAP enchanting/curses', the `minecraft:`-prefixed key form is the one already shipped in this pack's weaponry/trident_tag). `and_then_you_died` is a bare entity_killed_player. The two are ANDed as separate requirement groups. Honest limitations: vanilla has no player-death trigger, only entity_killed_player, so fall, lava, drowning and void deaths do not count — you must be killed by a mob or a player; and criteria are unordered, so dying first and being handed the cursed item afterwards also completes it.
- **Read the Room** — Pure criteria plus bucket lines, no detector. A player who places bookshelves raises minecraft.used:minecraft.bookshelf; that statistic is compared every medium tick against its own private mirror (bacaptmh_m1shelfm, seeded in seed_lines before any detector runs and refreshed UNGATED, so a lifetime placement total can never be read as a fresh stack) and a rise opens a 240-medium-tick, i.e. two-minute, window. When another player then fires the vanilla enchanted_item criterion, the bucket grants the advancement to every player 1..16 blocks away carrying a live window, then to the enchanter, then revokes the hook. distance=1..16 excludes the enchanter himself, so this cannot be self-served. Honest limits: the enchanter must still be standing by the table the stacker stocked, and the exact ring of fifteen bookshelves is NOT verified - there is no attested condition field for the level cost of an enchant (enchanted_item's `levels` is not used anywhere in the reference packs and I could not establish whether it carries the slot cost 1-3 or the displayed 1-30 requirement), so the description was reworded from 'fifteen bookshelves' to 'ringed with bookshelves' rather than promise a check that does not exist. A player who already holds this cannot help a newcomer, because a completed advancement stops latching its own criteria.
- **Repair Job** — Pure criteria, three of them ANDed. 'worn_out' is BACAP's Some Breakthrough trigger (item_durability_changed with an item predicate and a durability range) narrowed to #blazeandcave:pickaxes at 15 or fewer uses left. 'handed_over' fires when another player picks up a pickaxe you dropped and 'got_it_back' when you pick up a pickaxe another player dropped - both the diamonds_to_you / Fair Trade shape with the distance min 1 self-exclusion, and 'handed_over' alone already makes this impossible alone. What is NOT verified, and I am saying so rather than hiding it: nothing in the advancement system observes an anvil (there is no anvil trigger and no attested item damage sub-predicate in either reference pack), so the repair itself is inferred from the round trip. The description was rewritten from 'Repair another player's damaged tool on an Anvil' to match what actually fires.
- **Riptide Rampage** — Native PvP kill, no function. The riptide spin attack resolves through the normal player-attack path, so the killing blow carries #minecraft:is_player_attack. Riptide itself is identified by speed: BACAP's Hit and Run treats movement.speed 8.0 as a flat-out minecart, and speed is in blocks per second, so the 20.0 floor here is far above sprinting (~5.6), boats and minecarts (8) and horses (~14.5), while a Riptide I launch is roughly 30 and Riptide III roughly 60. An inverted equipment term rules out the one other way to be moving that fast in melee range — an elytra on your chest. Mainhand must be an actual Riptide-enchanted Trident. Stated uncertainty: I am confident about the damage type and the speed scale but have not been able to verify in-game that the spin attack's speed sample is still above 20 at the exact tick the victim dies; if it turns out to be marginal, the floor is the one number to lower.
- **Storm Chasers** — Detector, bucket_fast, gated on two or more players online and somebody still lacking it. A Riptide launch is read as the used:trident statistic ticking up in the same tick that SelectedItem still carries a minecraft:riptide enchantment - a thrown trident has left the hand by the next tick, a Riptide one has not - and stamps gametime into bacaptmh_sct. Once a stamped player is back on the ground (BACAP's blazeandcave:is_on_ground predicate) within ten seconds of launching, any other survival player within five blocks whose own stamp is inside 21 ticks of theirs and who is also on the ground gets tagged, and both are granted. Two limits I am stating rather than hiding: a trident used from the offhand is missed, because SelectedItem is the mainhand only; and the idea's 'same thunderstorm' clause is dropped, because a function here has no weather test (this generator emits functions, not predicate files, and BACAP ships no thundering predicate). The mirror is seeded in bacaptmh:seed and any delta above 20 is ignored, so a stale mirror cannot read a lifetime total as a launch.
- **Thorn in Your Side** — Four OR'd minecraft:player_killed_entity criteria, one per armour slot, so the Thorns can sit anywhere. There is no vanilla damage-type tag for thorns (Enhanced Discoveries had to define bacaped:is_thorns, which this pack must not depend on), so the damage is identified structurally instead: is_player_attack false, but both direct_entity and source_entity are players - which is what a thorns retaliation looks like and what arrows, potions, TNT, fire and falls are not, since their direct entity is the projectile or nothing. The residual risk is that a future version attributes thorns with a different direct entity, in which case this would stop firing rather than fire wrongly. No distance guard is needed: you cannot be the victim of your own player_killed_entity.

</details>
