<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Farm Share** — An AND of two pickup criteria: a full stack of Bread of yours picked up by another player, and a full stack of wheat, carrots, potatoes or beetroot of theirs picked up by you, both with the one-block self-exclusion. The 9x9 split field in the original idea needed per-block placement ownership and a maturity sweep - far beyond one detection function - so the advancement was rebuilt around the same fiction, two farmers trading their harvests, in pure criteria. The two halves need not be the same trade.
- **Golden Handshake** — Pure criteria, no detector. 'handed' is minecraft:thrown_item_picked_up_by_player - the trigger fires for the player who PICKS UP an item another entity threw, and its entity field is the thrower (BACAP's adventure/awards_ceremony uses exactly this shape with an Allay as the thrower). Pinning the thrower to entity_type player with distance min 1 is the diamonds_to_you self-exclusion idiom: a player who drops his own apple and walks back to it measures the distance from himself to himself, always 0, so he can never serve himself. 'eaten' is consume_item on the same item with an entity_scores check on bacaptmh_hp, a vanilla health objective, at 4 or below - two hearts. Requirements default to AND of both criteria. It is not verified that the apple eaten is the same apple that was handed over; both halves are required, and the receiving half is impossible alone.
- **Group Therapy** — Function-granted (detector 1 of 3). bucket_medium counts non-spectators into its own #gtn score — counted, never `if entity @a[limit=3]` — and only then enters detect/group_therapy, and only while somebody still lacks it. Eating a Golden Apple raises minecraft.used:minecraft.golden_apple; the detector compares that statistic against a private mirror, so a rise means an apple was eaten in the last 10 ticks. The health test is deliberately NOT read at detection time: a Golden Apple grants Absorption the instant it is swallowed and the vanilla health criterion counts absorption, so the eater would already read back above three hearts. Instead an ungated bucket line stamps bacaptmh_glow=4 on anyone currently at 5 health or less, and the detector requires that stamp to still be alive. A qualifying eater gets a 6-window (60 ticks); when three or more players hold a live window at once, all of them are granted. The mirror refresh, the low-health stamp and both window countdowns are ungated bucket lines that run whether or not the detector did, and the mirror is also seeded in seed_lines, so a lifetime apple total can never be read as a fresh event and a window can never freeze while the gate is shut. Known limit: proximity is not checked, so the three could be in different dimensions — the coordination is in the three-second window, not the geography.
- **Have Your Cake** — The 'bite' criterion is not invented: BACAP's own technical/castaway_fail.json detects a cake slice being eaten with exactly this structure - minecraft:default_block_use plus a location_check on minecraft:cake - alongside its consume_item criteria for every other food, so eating cake is proven to route through the empty-hand block-use path. An impossible guard sits in its own requirements group, so the latch alone never completes the advancement. Every medium tick the cake window is aged first, then anyone holding the latch runs detect/have_your_cake, which revokes the latch before anything else, opens a thirty-second window (60 medium ticks) on itself, and counts players whose own window is still open within six blocks using 'execute store result ... if entity' (a match COUNT, not the at-least-one test 'if entity @a[limit=4]' would give). At four it grants to all of them, not only whoever took the last slice. Six blocks is one player's reach on a cake plus slack, so it reads as one cake rather than a room of them; it cannot literally compare block positions, and two cakes side by side would also pass. gamemode=!creative is on both selectors because Player.canEat returns true for an invulnerable player, so a creative player can in fact eat cake.
- **Piece of Cake** — Pure criteria, no detector. The give/take pair is TMH adventure/fair_trade's exact shape, narrowed to minecraft:cake on both sides, so it is strictly harder than Fair Trade rather than a duplicate of it. Both guards pin the other party to entity_type player at distance min 1, which excludes drop-and-repick. Requirements default to AND, and there is no timing window - Fair Trade already owns the five-second swap. Reworded from 'two players each eat a slice of the same Cake': eating cake is a block interaction with no per-block sharing signal, so the original needed a detector watching bite states.
- **Potluck** — One detector (detect/m1_potluck). 'A different food' is expressed as five disjoint food families, each with its own consume_item criterion; the medium bucket turns each latch into its own 20-medium-tick (ten second) window plus a shared 'somebody ate' window, then revokes the latch, so nothing here reads a lifetime eating statistic. The detector runs at any player with a live shared window who lacks the advancement: it counts how many of the five family windows are alive within eight blocks, returns early unless all five are, then counts distinct eaters with `execute store result ... run execute if entity` and grants to all of them at five. requirements are an OR group over the five foods AND-ed with an impossible group, so the five criteria alone can never complete it. Known edge: five families and five eaters in range is checked, not a bijection, so one player who ate three different families alongside four others who ate the remaining two would also qualify.
- **Seed Exchange** — Pure criteria, no function - the shape of bacaptmh:adventure/fair_trade minus its detector. 'gave' is thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity (you threw it, a player picked it up); 'got' is thrown_item_picked_up_by_player (a player threw it, you picked it up); both carry the diamonds_to_you distance absolute min 1 guard, so dropping and re-collecting your own seeds satisfies neither. Default AND requirements. Deliberate change from the suggested mechanism: the five-second pairing window is dropped, because enforcing it would cost a detector this batch cannot afford. The consequence is honest and small - the give and the receive need not be the same trade, and since both items are in one list the pair could in principle be the same species both ways. Both items are Sniffer-exclusive, so ancient seeds still had to move in both directions between two people.
- **Soup Kitchen** — Seven copies of thrown_item_picked_up_by_player, one per foodLevel from 0 to 6, because entity predicates have no hunger field and NBT matching is exact - the seven sit in one OR group, so any hunger at or below three shanks satisfies it. Each demands a real food item and a thrower who is a player at distance min 1. The second group is a consume_item on the same food list. Deliberately checked at PICKUP time rather than at eating time: it is not settled whether consume_item fires before or after the food is applied, and being starving when the parcel arrives is the unambiguous reading. The eaten item is not proven to be the item you were given.
- **Stew Roulette** — Written from the eater's side so it is expressible as pure criteria: the stew must have been picked up off another player's drop (thrower entity_type player, distance min 1) and then eaten, with consume_item lifted verbatim from BACAP suspicious_looking_stew. No detector. The 'catch the effect' clause is dropped — effects_changed cannot tell that the effect came from a stew as opposed to any other source — but eating a Suspicious Stew always applies its effect anyway, so nothing is lost in practice. Only the eater is granted; the cook is not, which matches how criteria-only entries such as First Blood behave.

</details>

| · | **Golden Seam** | Mining | Take a Silk Touched Nether Gold Ore block from another player (2 players) | 15 |
| · | **Hard Hat Handover** | Mining | Hand another player an Iron Helmet and take a Diamond Helmet from them in return (2 players) | 15 |
| ◆ | **Homing Pigeon** | Mining | Be handed a Compass by another player and use it on a Lodestone at least 64 blocks away (2 players) | 90 |
| · | **Lantern Light** | Mining | Kill another player under the glow berries of a Lush Cave (2 players) | 15 |
| ◆ | **Loan Shark** | Mining | Lend another player a Diamond Pickaxe and take it back worn past half its life (2 players) | 90 |
| · | **Miner's Lamp** | Mining | Be given Night Vision by another player while you are below Y=-40 (2 players) | 15 |
| · | **Mineshaft Mixtape** | Mining | Take a Music Disc off another player below Y=0 (2 players) | 15 |
| · | **Outsourced** | Mining | Craft a Diamond Pickaxe from three Diamonds another player handed you (2 players) | 15 |
| · | **Oxidised Opponent** | Mining | Kill another player wearing a full set of Copper armour (2 players) | 15 |
| · | **Passing the Torch** | Mining | Place a Copper Torch that another player handed to you (2 players) | 15 |
| ★ | **Patina Pipeline** | Mining | Take every oxidation stage of Copper Block and its waxed twin from other players (2 players) | 120 |
| ◆ | **Pick of the Bunch** | Mining | Be handed a Pickaxe of all seven materials by other players (2 players) | 40 |
| ★ | **Quarry Crew** | Mining | Take a full stack of Cobblestone, Stone, Deepslate, Granite, Diorite, Andesite and Tuff from other players (2 players) | 120 |
| · | **Run It Into the Ground** | Mining | Take a Shovel off another player and dig with it until it snaps (2 players) | 15 |
| · | **Sealed With Wax** | Mining | Take a Copper Block from another player and seal it with Honeycomb (2 players) | 15 |
| · | **Second Set of Eyes** | Mining | Scout ahead through a Spyglass that another player handed you (2 players) | 15 |

| ◆ | **Sixty Below** | Mining | Kill another player at the very bottom of the world, both of you below Y=-60 (2 players) | 40 |
| ◆ | **Split Shift** | Mining | Hand a full stack of sixty-four Iron Ingots straight into another player's hands (2 players) | 45 |
| · | **Stalactite Standoff** | Mining | Run another player through with a Spear in a Dripstone Cave (2 players) | 15 |
| · | **Statuary Trade** | Mining | Be handed a Copper Golem Statue by another player and stand it up (2 players) | 15 |
| ◆ | **Stripped to the Studs** | Mining | Wear a Diamond Chestplate down to nothing under another player's blows (2 players) | 40 |

| · | **Vault Transfer** | Mining | Take a Shulker Box off another player below Y=-50 (2 players) | 15 |

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Bring a Torch** — One criterion, no detector. The pickup half is the fair_trade 'got' shape — thrower entity_type player at distance at least 1, which self-excludes because the thrower of your own drop is measured against itself and is always at distance 0. The darkness half is the light predicate straight out of BACAP mining/heart_of_darkness, nested in the generic 'player' entity predicate that every criterion accepts. Light 0 is the combined block-and-sky brightness, so an open surface at midnight (moonlight leaves it at about 4) does not qualify; a cave or a sealed room does. Being a single criterion, requirements is left empty and the default all-criteria rule applies.
- **Deep Recovery** — Pure criteria, AND-ed, no detector. A player's death drops carry that player as their Thrower, so picking one up fires thrown_item_picked_up_by_player with `entity` bound to the dead owner - which is why entity_type player plus distance min 1 is enough to mean 'somebody else's belongings' and can never be your own drop. The y-bound uses the exact location/position shape BACAP uses in end/the_void_frogs and end/uh_houston_we_have_a_problem. The return leg is the mirrored trigger (thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity, the diamonds_to_you idiom) fired for the giver with the picker pinned to a player at least a block away, above Y=0. Stated honestly: the trigger cannot tell a death drop from a deliberate toss, and nothing checks that the items handed back are the same items or that the recipient is the same player - what is enforced is a genuine two-player exchange, one leg below Y=-50 and one above ground.
- **Dig Me Out** — Detector bacaptmh:detect/dig_me_out, called unconditionally from bucket_medium. Its first three lines keep a private mirror of every non-spectator's minecraft.mined:minecraft.gravel and turn any increase into a 3-window (1.5 s) bacaptmh_gravt flag; they are deliberately ungated so a mirror can never freeze while somebody is offline and then read a lifetime total as a fresh dig, and the mirror itself is created in bacaptmh:seed. The state machine then runs as/at each survival player who still lacks the advancement: while the block at ~ ~1 ~ is gravel and another player is within 6 blocks it stamps bacaptmh_bury 3; when that head block is gone, the victim's own gravel counter did NOT move, and a different player within 6 blocks has a live gravel flag, both the victim and every qualifying digger are granted. Requiring the victim's own gravel delta to be zero is what stops someone from digging themselves out and claiming a rescue. Attribution is by proximity plus mined-gravel statistic rather than a real block-break hook, so a bystander who happened to be mining gravel a few blocks away in the same 1.5 s could be credited alongside the real digger.
- **Homing Pigeon** — 'handed' is a Compass picked up from another player's throw. 'arrived' is the exact criterion of vanilla adventure/use_lodestone (item_used_on_block with a location_check on lodestone and a match_tool on compass) - you have to be standing at the Lodestone and right-click it, which is the arrival proof. Both are required. The original idea stored the Lodestone's coordinates and watched the recipient close on them; that needed a detector, so instead the recipient's own use of the compass ON the Lodestone stands in for reaching it. Nothing forces the Lodestone to be the one the compass was locked to, or the one the giver placed.
- **Loan Shark** — Three AND'd criteria: a Diamond Pickaxe of yours picked up by another player, a Diamond Pickaxe of theirs picked up by you, and a durability change on a Diamond Pickaxe leaving it at 780 or less of its 1561 - the `durability` field of item_durability_changed, used as a bare int in BACAP's end/some_breakthrough and taken here as a bounded range. Stamping the actual item with the borrower's wear would have needed custom_data and a detector; this keeps the story (it went out, it came back, it is half dead) without tracking item identity, so a player who lends one pickaxe and is handed back a different one still qualifies.
- **Outsourced** — Pure criteria, AND-ed. 'diamonds' needs a single dropped stack of three or more diamonds picked up from another player — item.count is the ItemPredicate field BACAP uses in end/what_for, and because 'entity' on this trigger is the thrower, a self-drop sits at distance 0 and is excluded. 'pickaxe' is minecraft:recipe_crafted on recipe_id minecraft:diamond_pickaxe, the shape BACAP uses in monsters/boned. Not enforced: that the three diamonds you were given are the three that went into the pickaxe.
- **Passing the Torch** — Pure criteria, AND of two groups. 'handed' is thrown_item_picked_up_by_player on minecraft:copper_torch with entity_type player and the distance min 1 self-exclusion guard, so the torch must come out of somebody else's hand. 'placed' is the placed_block block-predicate shape copied verbatim from blazeandcave:building/chartreuse, including both block forms a placed copper torch can take (copper_torch standing, copper_wall_torch on a side). Both criteria land on the receiver, so this is the receiver's advancement; the ten-second window from the original idea is not enforced, because criteria carry no timing relationship, and the description no longer claims one.
- **Silent Duel** — Pure criteria, no function - the suggested shrieker-counting detector is replaced by its observable consequence. A Sculk Shrieker's activation is what applies Darkness to every player within 40 blocks for 12 seconds, so 'neither of us has Darkness at the moment of the kill' is the criteria-expressible form of 'nobody set a Shrieker off'. Both the killer (player) and the victim (entity) are given as lists of loot conditions: an entity_properties term pinning location.biomes to minecraft:deep_dark (the field shape verified against monsters/warden_frostbite), ANDed with a minecraft:inverted term over an effects predicate for minecraft:darkness (the inverted/effects shapes verified against enchanting/silent_but_deadly and end/dogfight). No self-exclusion guard is needed: player_killed_entity fires for the killer and a player cannot be their own killed entity. A nearby Warden also applies Darkness and would block it - which is correct, since a Warden means Shriekers went off.
- **Split Shift** — Pure criteria, no detector, one criterion so no custom requirements are needed. thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity fires on the GIVER when the stack is picked up; `count: 64` is the exact-integer item-predicate form BACAP uses in enchanting/magical_stockpile, and a dropped full stack arrives as one 64-count item entity. The picker is pinned to entity_type player with the distance min 1 self-exclusion, so picking your own drop back up does nothing. Reframed from the original 'fuel a Blast Furnace, let someone pull 64 ingots out of it': no vanilla trigger reports furnace fuelling or output extraction, so that version would have cost a container-polling detector for a goal-tier entry.
- **Tunnel Vision** — Detector, bucket_medium. There is no aggregate 'blocks mined' statistic, so mined:stone + mined:deepslate + mined:tuff are summed by hand and differenced against a mirror seeded in bacaptmh:seed; any window delta over 200 is discarded as a stale mirror rather than trusted as a burst of digging. The first block of a dig records that dig head's x/z, and ten seconds with nothing broken ends the dig and zeroes its tally, so the 150-block threshold means 150 blocks in one continuous session. When a player is below y=50 with 150 in the current dig and the nearest other survival player within three blocks also has 150, the squared horizontal distance between the two dig heads is computed - both axis deltas clamped to 1000 before squaring so the multiply cannot overflow a 32-bit score - and both are granted at 200 blocks or more. What is enforced is where each tunnel started and where the two of them met, not that the last block broken was literally the wall between them.

</details>

## Monsters — Too Many Hands

| | Advancement | Tab | What you do | XP |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| · | **Assist** | Monsters | Finish off a monster that a teammate had already beaten down below two hearts (2 players) | 15 |
| · | **Avenged** | Monsters | Shoot down a monster that has locked onto another player at least eight blocks away (2 players) | 55 |
| ★ | **Bait and Switch** | Monsters | Loot an Ancient City chest while a Warden is chasing another player at least thirty blocks away (2 players) | 160 |
| · | **Beggars and Choosers** | Monsters | Choke down Rotten Flesh another player handed you (2 players) | 15 |
| ◆ | **Bomb Disposal** | Monsters | Put a Creeper down while it is about to go off in another player's face (2 players) | 60 |
| · | **Bounty Hunter** | Monsters | Get paid in Emeralds by another player within a minute of hunting a monster down (2 players) | 20 |
| ◆ | **Curse of the Deep** | Monsters | Cut down another player inside an Ocean Monument while the Elder Guardian's Mining Fatigue is on them (2 players) | 40 |
| · | **Death Do Us Part** | Monsters | Have two players killed by Creepers in the same instant, in the same place (2 players) | 30 |
| ◆ | **Fight in the Dark** | Monsters | Take a hit from another player in the Deep Dark while the Darkness is pressing in on you (2 players) | 40 |
| · | **Guardian Angel** | Monsters | Cut down in melee a monster that was hunting another player (2 players) | 50 |
| · | **Heads Up** | Monsters | Hand another player a mob head (2 players) | 15 |
| ◆ | **Heart Surgery** | Monsters | Smash a Creaking Heart while its Creaking is fixated on a different player (2 players) | 150 |
| ◆ | **Hold the Line** | Monsters | Block a Ravager's charge with your shield while a teammate kills it from behind (2 players) | 55 |
| ★ | **Human Shield** | Monsters | Bring down a Warden while it is locked on to another player instead of you (2 players) | 200 |
| · | **Hunger Pangs** | Monsters | Be struck by another player while Hunger is gnawing at you (2 players) | 15 |
| ◆ | **Last Rites** | Monsters | Take a Totem of Undying off another player while you are down to three hearts (2 players) | 40 |
| ★ | **Last Stand** | Monsters | Kill a Warden after every other player who joined the fight has already died (3 players) | 300 |
| ◆ | **Lend Me Your Axe** | Monsters | Chop down a Ravager with an Axe another player handed you (2 players) | 40 |
| · | **Night Watch** | Monsters | Have another player kill a monster within ten blocks of you while you sleep (2 players) | 30 |
| ★ | **Noble Sacrifice** | Monsters | Be killed by the Wither in a fight that a teammate goes on to win (2 players) | 140 |
| · | **Pincer Movement** | Monsters | Have two players hit the same monster from opposite sides at the same moment (2 players) | 25 |
| ◆ | **Taking One For The Team** | Monsters | Die beside a teammate who is down to half a heart, and have them live out the next minute (2 players) | 140 |
| ◆ | **Total Party Kill** | Monsters | Wipe out: all three or more players on the server dead inside the same ten seconds (3 players) | 40 |
| · | **Trial by Fire** | Monsters | Set three players alight at once and have all three put themselves out and live (3 players) | 30 |
| ◆ | **Warded in the Dark** | Monsters | Be given Resistance by another player in the Deep Dark while a Shrieker's Darkness is on you (2 players) | 40 |
| ★ | **Wither Committee** | Monsters | Bring down a Wither with three or more players standing within twenty blocks (3 players) | 180 |

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Assist** — Every 0.5 s each player whose damage_dealt delta is positive stamps bacaptmh_dmgt with the gametime and walks the monsters within 12 blocks. The walk is #blazeandcave:hostile_monsters MINUS the three bosses that tag also contains (Ender Dragon, Wither, Warden): the tag keeps armour stands, boats, item frames, villagers and farm animals out of the search, and the three exclusions keep Assist about ordinary mobs. Without them this goal would have been a free side effect of Tag Team, Wither Committee and Warden Party in this same batch - a boss lingers at 0 health through a long death animation, necessarily passes through 0.1-3.9 HP under a 10-tick sampler, and those three advancements already guarantee two or more co-located players with fresh damage deltas in range - and 'beaten down below two hearts' reads absurdly for a 200-HP dragon. A monster read between 0.1 and 3.9 health gets the entity tag bacaptmh_softened, an entity tag rather than a scoreboard row, so it vanishes with the mob and nothing can leak. When a monster carrying that tag is next seen at 0 health, the finisher's own sample counts how many non-spectator, non-creative players within 16 blocks dealt damage in the last 100 ticks; at two or more, all of them are granted and the tag is removed. The health read is now a single data get, with the shared temp score reset to a sentinel first instead of an 'unless data entity @s Health' probe, so the walk costs one NBT access per mob per damaging player rather than two while still being safe against a read that returns nothing. There is no per-mob identity scoreboard at all, so the pid/soft/softby objectives and their permanent UUID rows are gone. Attribution is still by statistic delta plus proximity rather than by a damage-source hook, so in a dense melee a second attacker who was hitting a different mob nearby can be credited alongside the real pair.
- **Bait and Switch** — DETECTOR 3 of 5. `looted` is player_generates_container_loot on minecraft:chests/ancient_city, the exact shape BACAP challenges/i_am_loot uses for every other chest table; the impossible criterion is a separate requirements group so the loot flag alone can never finish it. bucket_fast runs detect/bait_and_switch as any player holding {looted=true,impossible=false}. Because a Warden's target is null for whole ticks at a time, the flag is NOT consumed on the first pass: bacaptmh_baitw counts the passes and only revokes after 200 of them (ten seconds of grace). Each pass walks @e[type=warden,distance=..96,limit=4,sort=nearest] `on target at @s`; the target qualifies if it is a non-spectator player other than the looter and the looter is 30 or more blocks away, at which point both are granted. Honest limitation: `execute on target` is vanilla grammar but is not attested in any installed pack, and a Warden that has just lost aggro reads as no target at all.
- **Bomb Disposal** — Detector, bucket_medium, dispatched straight onto the Creepers within 12 blocks of any player who lacks it (limit=6, sort=nearest, so the selector is bounded). It reads the Creeper's Health and does nothing unless it is zero - that is the whole trick, because a Creeper that detonates is discarded outright with its health intact, so zero health only ever means it was killed, and a killed mob lingers through a 20-tick death animation that a 10-tick sampler cannot miss. It then requires a player inside 3.5 blocks, the range at which a Creeper is actually swelling, and two or more non-spectators within 12 blocks, and grants all of them - the saved and the saviour alike, since Cooperative Mode is off. The fuse is inferred from that proximity: swelling is not persisted in NBT (only flint-and-steel ignition sets 'ignited'), so there is no way to read a natural hiss directly.
- **Bounty Hunter** — Two vanilla criteria ANDed: picking Emeralds off another player's drop with the distance-min-1 thrower guard, and a player_killed_entity on the existing #blazeandcave:hostile_monsters entity tag. No detector, and therefore no five-second link between the kill and the payment — the two latch independently, so in practice this reads as 'be paid in Emeralds by a teammate at some point after you have killed a monster'. That is deliberate: tying the two together would have cost a detector for a task-tier entry, and the multiplayer requirement lives entirely in the payment half, which no solo player can produce.
- **Death Do Us Part** — The death itself is a pure criterion - entity_killed_player with entity_type creeper, exactly BACAP monsters/aww_man - and requirements are [[creeper],[impossible]], an AND of groups, so it never completes on its own. bucket_fast runs every tick: it first decays bacaptmh_dup, then stamps 6 on anyone carrying the flag and revokes the flag immediately, so the criterion can never latch and re-fire. detect/death_pair runs at each stamped player and grants to every stamped player within 16 blocks once a real head count finds two or more. One shared creeper is approximated by 'two creeper deaths inside six ticks and sixteen blocks' rather than by comparing the creeper's UUID, which commands cannot do; with doImmediateRespawn on both victims are at spawn together, which the same test still accepts.
- **Guardian Angel** — One criterion, no function. targeted_entity is the field Enhanced Discoveries uses in Save Me! and Suspicious Duel, so the mob's current attack target can be tested directly: it must be a minecraft:player, and distance is measured from the killer's own position, so a mob hunting the killer sits at distance 0 and is excluded by min 1 - the target has to be somebody else. Restricted to #blazeandcave:hostile_monsters so it cannot be satisfied by a tamed wolf or an iron golem. Stated uncertainty: this relies on the mob still holding its target when player_killed_entity fires during LivingEntity.die. I believe it does, because the trigger runs before the entity is discarded, but I have not been able to confirm it from the reference packs, and if the target is cleared first the advancement would simply never fire rather than fire wrongly. The 'below three hearts' clause of the original idea was dropped: no attested entity predicate exposes health, so it would have cost a detection function.
- **Heads Up** — One criterion, the giver's side of the handoff: thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity fires on the thrower with 'this' bound to whoever picked the drop up, which is exactly how BACAP technical/give_piglin_gold detects a piglin taking your gold. The picker is pinned to a player at distance min 1, the diamonds_to_you guard - your own drop is always at distance 0 from you, so a projectile-style self-pickup cannot satisfy it, and a mob cannot either. minecraft:player_head is deliberately NOT in the list: building/trophy_wall already covers passing a player's own head around, and leaving it out keeps this strictly about mob heads. No detector.
- **Heart Surgery** — Pure criteria, no function — and the reason it works is BACAP itself: blazeandcave:biomes/heart_breaker ('Smash a Creaking Heart to destroy its Creaking') is implemented as player_killed_entity on entity_type creaking, which proves the breaker is credited with the Creaking's death. This adds the role split with targeted_entity — the same nested field Enhanced Discoveries uses in Save Me! and Dinner Time — requiring the dying Creaking's target to be a player at least 4 blocks from the breaker. Nested distance is measured from the same origin as the outer predicate (the breaker's position), so a target 4+ blocks away cannot be the breaker himself; alone, the Creaking would be hunting you and the check fails. Stated uncertainty: this assumes the Creaking's target is still set on the tick it is torn down by the heart breaking, which I could not verify from the shipped packs.
- **Hold the Line** — No detector; two vanilla criteria paired in the medium bucket. `blocked` is BACAP monsters/blast_shield's damage predicate with the creeper swapped for a Ravager; when it latches, the bucket opens a 10-medium-tick (five second) window and immediately revokes the hook, so a block survived last week cannot be paired with today's kill. `slew` latches on the finisher. The grant lines run `at` the finisher and target @a[distance=1..24,...] with a live block window - distance=1..24 excludes the finisher himself, so one player who both blocked and killed gets nothing. The tank is granted first, then the finisher, then the `slew` hook is revoked; the revoke line is gated on impossible=false so it can never un-complete a finished advancement. requirements are three AND-ed groups, one of them impossible, so the criteria alone never complete it. Honest limit: identity of the Ravager is not tracked - the pairing is proximity (24 blocks) plus the five-second window, so in a raid with two Ravagers the block and the kill could in principle be on different animals.
- **Human Shield** — minecraft:player_killed_entity on a Warden whose `targeted_entity` is a player at least four blocks from the killer; a Warden aggroed on the killer measures 0 and is excluded, so somebody else must be holding its attention. The sonic-boom geometry in the original idea is not expressible - a boom only ever damages the Warden's current target, so 'take the hit meant for someone else' cannot be detected - and the ray cast it would need was not worth a detection function. Honest caveat: this pack's existing Warden Party is likely to be earned in the same fight, since three players around a Warden usually means it is looking at one of the other two.
- **Last Stand** — 'slew' arms the check and the impossible guard, in its own requirement group, stops it completing on criteria alone. detect/last_stand then runs every tick for the killer and reads bacaptmh_dtime, the per-player death stamp detect/sample keeps, against #f3cut1200 (the 60-second cutoff computed in the medium bucket, so up to nine ticks stale here). It grants when at least two OTHER players died inside that minute and every player within 64 blocks of the killer is one of them - i.e. nobody is left standing beside him. The fallen are granted too. 'Joined the fight' is approximated by 'died in the last minute'; a teammate who died elsewhere in the world at the same time would also count.
- **Night Watch** — Criteria latch the two halves, a detector pairs them. requirements is an AND of three single-criterion groups including the impossible 'guard', so no lone player can ever complete it from criteria — that grouping is deliberate, one group containing all three would be an OR and would defeat the guard. bacaptmh:detect/night_watch runs from bucket_medium whenever 2 or more players are online and at least one still lacks it: it stamps a 20-window (10 s) timer on anyone holding 'slept' or 'killed' and immediately revokes that criterion, so neither half can sit latched for days waiting for the other. Any sleeper with a live timer who has a player with a live kill timer within 1..10 blocks grants to both. #blazeandcave:hostile_monsters is the same entity tag detect/assist walks, and entity_type accepts a tag exactly as blazeandcave:monsters/keep_your_distance does. The sleeper is identified by the slept_in_bed trigger rather than by live sleeping state, which I could not test for without an NBT field I had no shipped example of; in practice the 10 s window covers the night skip.
- **Noble Sacrifice** — Half criteria, half detector. 'fell' is entity_killed_player with entity_type wither — the same shape BACAP uses for the Warden — and the impossible guard sits in its own requirements group so the flag alone never completes it. detect/noble_sacrifice consumes 'fell' with an advancement revoke (the field_medic idiom) and converts it into a 120-window (60 s), so a death from months ago can never be paid out by an unrelated Wither kill. The avenger is whoever's minecraft.killed:minecraft.wither rose in that window; he is tagged and excluded from the payout, so dying to your own Wither and then finishing it yourself is rejected. Only the fallen are granted — deliberately, since the advancement describes the dead player's role and granting the avenger would reopen the solo path.
- **Pincer Movement** — Function-granted by detect/pincer, dispatched every medium tick (gated on two or more online, and on at least one player still missing it) as each non-spectator, non-creative player whose bacaptmh_dmgd delta is positive in this window - the same damage-statistic delta detect/sample already maintains for Assist, which is ungated and self-seeding, so an arriving player's lifetime damage_dealt can never be mistaken for a hit. The detector considers only the single nearest hostile monster within 4.5 blocks, so two attackers can never be matched across two different mobs, tags every fresh damager within 4.5 blocks of that mob, and grants to each tagged player who has another tagged player at least 7 blocks away. 'Opposite sides' is that geometry rather than a yaw or dot-product calculation: both attackers inside 4.5 blocks of the mob while at least 7 blocks apart puts the mob between them at an angle of roughly 100 degrees or more. Attribution is by damage-statistic delta plus proximity, not by a damage-source hook, so in a dense melee a player who hit a different mob in the same half second can be credited; the tag is cleared at the end of every pass.
- **Taking One For The Team** — Function-granted, in three phases. MARK: every medium tick each eligible player's Health is read once at scale 10, and anyone reading 1..10 (0.1 to 1.0 HP, i.e. half a heart or less, with a dead player's true 0 excluded) writes their own player id and the gametime onto every eligible player within 8 blocks, in two single commands. Marking from the LIVING player's position is the point: nothing is ever measured from where a corpse happens to be, so this works with doImmediateRespawn on. OPEN: a player whose death stamp and whose mark are both under 100 ticks old, and whose death has not already been processed, opens a 120-window (60 s) watch on the ally named by the stored player id - matched by score, never by substituting a name into a selector. WATCH: the watch is cancelled outright if the ally picks up a fresh death stamp. PAY: when it runs out, the advancement is granted to the survivor AND, by player id, to the player who went down beside them - Cooperative Mode is off, so both participants are paid. Known softness: the mark is whoever was last on half a heart within 8 blocks in that window, so in a crowded fight with two dying players the credited pair can be the wrong one of two adjacent candidates.
- **Total Party Kill** — Deaths are detected from a vanilla deathCount objective (bacaptmh_deaths) that detect/sample reads and immediately resets every window, stamping bacaptmh_dtime with the gametime; sitting on the death screen stamps it too, as a second path. That means the wipe is still detected on servers running doImmediateRespawn, where nobody ever occupies the death screen, and it cannot be missed by a player who respawns between two samples. The check counts online non-spectator, non-creative players and how many of them carry a stamp newer than 200 ticks; when the two counts are equal and there are at least three players, everyone is granted. A counter that is consumed rather than compared against a saved lifetime total cannot produce a first-run false positive.
- **Trial by Fire** — Function-granted. Two UNGATED bucket_medium lines read every non-spectator's Fire NBT into bacaptmh_fire with 'execute store result ... run data get entity @s Fire' — ungated so a player who already holds the advancement still counts towards somebody else's three, and refreshed every pass so no burning state is ever read from a stale score. detect/trial_by_fire then counts burning players within 16 blocks; at three it arms all of them for 20 medium ticks (ten seconds) and stamps their current deathCount into bacaptmh_tbfd. It pays out when three armed players in that radius are all at Fire <= 0 and all still carry the same deathCount they were armed with — that is the 'and live' half, and it is done with a deathCount objective seeded in seed_lines rather than with the shared bacaptmh_dtime, whose score is simply absent for a player who has never died and would have made the advancement unobtainable for them. Grants only to the three who actually burned and survived.
- **Wither Committee** — Gated on three or more non-spectators actually being online (counted properly into #players bacaptmh_sys, not with a limit= selector, which only ever tests for one match). Players who lack the advancement and have a Wither within 20 blocks run a check as the nearest Wither; it reads the Wither's Health NBT and, once Health is 0 (the Wither persists through its long death animation before dropping the Nether Star), counts non-spectator, non-creative players within 20 blocks who are not on the death screen and grants to all of them if there are at least three. Counting is never gated on who already holds the advancement, so a player who already has it still counts toward the three and cannot lock anybody out.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Air Mail** — Fires on the sender: thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity is the trigger BACAP uses for Diamonds to you!, and it carries the picker-upper in `entity`. The delivery only counts if the picker is a player riding a Happy Ghast at least a block away and the sender is also mounted on one, above y=150 (the location.position.y form BACAP uses in Sky High). Substitution note: the original asked for a five-hundred-block unbroken flight; ride-distance-since-mount is state, and state means a detector, so altitude stands in for the journey — you cannot be at y 150 on a Happy Ghast without having flown there, and the ghast is the only way to hold that altitude while trading items. Known limit: two players seated on the *same* Happy Ghast also satisfy it, since seat separation exceeds a block; it is still a two-player act, just an easier one.
- **Air Rescue** — Function-granted. detect/air_rescue runs every tick for survival players without the advancement, but only while a Happy Ghast is within 24 blocks, which is what keeps a per-tick NBT read affordable - the same gating trust_fall uses. It stores FallDistance into a score; passing 50 opens a 100-tick window. The grant fires when that window is open, FallDistance has dropped back to 0 (the fall ended), the faller is alive, and a Happy Ghast within 6 blocks has a player passenger who is not the faller; both the faller and everyone aboard the ghast are granted. Two honest uncertainties: whether a player who lands on a Happy Ghast's back is caught by it at all, and whether the FallDistance NBT path still carries that name - a rename would make this silently never fire rather than break anything.
- **Anchor Management** — 'charged' is BACAP building/shelf_expression's exact item_used_on_block shape - a location_check for the block and a match_tool for the stack, which is the only place that trigger exposes the item - pointed at minecraft:respawn_anchor and minecraft:glowstone. 'delivery' is the diamonds_to_you thrower guard on Glowstone. The original wanted the anchor's charger compared against the player who later respawns on it; there is no respawn trigger at all in vanilla and block ownership is not tracked anywhere in this pack, so the idea is flipped to the half the game can see - the fuel came from somebody else and you burned it into the anchor. The two criteria latch independently, so the Glowstone you were handed need not be the exact block you fed the anchor.
- **Bastion Buddy** — Pure criteria, no detector. 'kitted_out' is thrown_item_picked_up_by_player on a Golden Helmet whose thrower is a player at distance min 1 - the exact self-exclusion idiom BACAP uses in mining/diamonds_to_you, and the thrower of your own drop always measures 0 from you, so it can only be somebody else. 'treasure' is player_generates_container_loot on minecraft:chests/bastion_treasure (the same trigger/loot_table shape as BACAP mining/frigid_diversions and biomes/x_marks_the_spot), narrowed with a player equipment predicate on the head slot - the same equipment field vanilla's repair_wolf_armor uses. Gold armour is what makes Piglins ignore you, so the story the criteria actually enforce is 'a teammate handed you the disguise and you walked into the treasure room in it'. Substitution stated plainly: the original wanted two Piglin Brutes targeting a different player, and player_generates_container_loot exposes only the looter and the loot table - there is no way to look at nearby mobs from inside it. The two criteria latch independently and are not time-linked, the same looseness Pay It Forward accepts.
- **Beacon of Hope** — Pure criteria, two ANDed. 'built_it' is recipe_crafted with recipe_id minecraft:beacon, the same trigger and field BACAP potion/imbued_projectiles uses for minecraft:tipped_arrow, and beacon is a listed id. 'handed_the_star' is the distance-min-1 thrower guard on a Nether Star, which has exactly one source in survival - a Wither - so the second player really did pay for it. No detector, and no time link between the two criteria, which is the same looseness the rest of the pack's supply-chain entries accept.
- **Blood Brothers** — Function-granted. A private deathCount objective (bacaptmh_bbd, seeded to 0 on first sight and consumed every medium tick so it can never leak) opens a 40-medium-tick — twenty second — window on any player who dies. detect/blood_brothers runs for players who have that window open, are off the death screen again (nbt=!{Health:0.0f}) and are standing in the Nether; it counts the others within 8 blocks in the same state and grants to all of them at four. The Respawn Anchor is not read as a block: it is inferred, because an anchor is the only thing in the game that can put you back on your feet inside the Nether, and running back through a portal inside twenty seconds of dying is not a realistic alternative. Everyone who qualifies is granted, not just whoever tripped the check. Online players are counted into #mp6players with an explicit tally rather than an 'if entity @a[limit=4]' test.
- **Changing of the Guard** — One started_riding criterion (BACAP uses this trigger 25 times with exactly this player/vehicle shape). At the moment it fires you are already in the ghast's passenger list, so the passenger sub-predicate would match you — the distance min 1 guard rejects that (self measures 0) and only a second rider, who sits roughly two blocks away on a happy ghast, satisfies it. location.position.y min 150 is the same form BACAP uses for its altitude checks. Substituted mechanism, stated openly: criteria cannot observe seat index changing hands, so this is 'board a ghast someone else is already flying' rather than a pilot swap; it needs the same two people in the air and no function.
- **Corporate Raiders** — SWAPPED IN for 'Look Away', which is dead twice over: a Creaking's freeze-when-observed rule is invisible to criteria (there is no line-of-sight predicate for a second player), and the idea is already authored as bacaptmh:monsters/heart_surgery, 'Smash a Creaking Heart while its Creaking is fixated on a different player'. Corporate Raiders is a spare and it is fully native. Both criteria use thrown_item_picked_up_by_player, the trigger BACAP itself uses for bartering in hung_drawn_and_bartered, and both require the player to be standing on a bastion_remnant structure piece, the same location.structures form BACAP uses in raidin_master and thanks_a_lotl. 'grubstake' is a gold ingot picked up off a thrower who is entity_type minecraft:player at distance.absolute.min 1 -- your own dropped gold puts the thrower at distance 0 and fails, so this is strictly another player's gold. 'the_payoff' is the barter itself: an item picked up off a minecraft:piglin, item type unconstrained so any barter return counts. Both are required, so the advancement is impossible alone even though the barter half is trivially solo. Two honest caveats. First, 'structures' matches only when you are inside an actual generated piece of the bastion, not merely within its bounding box, so a raider standing on the lava moat outside will not latch it -- both halves have to happen in the ruin proper. Second, the original wanted two raiders bartering within ten seconds and coming away with different items; neither the timing nor the item comparison is expressible, so what ships is 'your partner staked you and you spent it in their bastion'. The title still fits; the ten-second window is gone.
- **Dead Drop** — One criterion. thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity fires on the thrower when anything picks the drop up (BACAP technical/give_piglin_gold), and the loot context's origin is the thrower's own position, so distance min 100 on the picker means you were a hundred blocks off when they took it - a guard that self-excludes far harder than the usual min 1. Both sides are pinned to minecraft:the_nether. Retitled from Portal Post, and here is exactly why, because it is the one thing I checked and could not make safe: the item's thrower is resolved through the level it is currently in, so an item that goes through a Nether portal and is picked up in a different dimension from its thrower most likely resolves to no owner at all and the trigger never fires - which would have made the spare's version silently unobtainable. Same-dimension distance is the version I can defend. The item entity's five-minute despawn is the real time limit.
- **Emergency Exit** — changed_dimension nether-to-overworld (the structure Enhanced Discoveries uses in from_nether_to_over) latches, an impossible guard in its own group keeps criteria alone from completing it, and detect/emergency_exit revokes the latch, opens a ten-medium-tick (five second) window on that player and reads their Health at scale 10, flagging them when it is 1..39 — under two hearts, with a dead player's 0 excluded. It then looks for another player inside 1..16 blocks with an open window, requiring at least one of the pair to carry the low-health flag, and grants to both. distance=1..16 excludes the executor from their own search. The teammate-lit-portal clause from the original idea was dropped: block-place ownership is not tracked anywhere in this pack and would have cost a second detector. One known looseness: respawning from the Nether into the Overworld also fires changed_dimension, so a teammate who died could stand in for one who walked through, though they would have to land within 16 blocks of the portal mouth.
- **Escort Service** — Detection function on the medium bucket. A non-spectator, non-creative player who lacks it accumulates their own walk_one_cm + sprint_one_cm delta while, in the same sample, they are in the Nether, every armour, hotbar, inventory and hand slot is empty, and at least one other non-spectator player is within 16 blocks; any damage_taken in the sample, or any failed condition, zeroes the run. All three statistic mirrors are seeded in bacaptmh:seed, never in the bucket, so a first sample cannot read a lifetime total as one step. At 50000 cm the escorted player and every other non-spectator within 16 blocks are granted.
- **Ghast Fleet** — Players within 24 blocks are stepped onto their vehicle; each Happy Ghast that turns out to be carrying a non-spectator, non-creative player and sits within 20 blocks is tagged, and the tagged ghasts are counted. At four or more distinct crewed ghasts every player riding one of them is granted, which is what makes this different from Enhanced Discoveries' Ghast Squad (four players on a single ghast). Tags are cleared at both ends of the function so nothing leaks between checks. The description no longer promises 'airborne' because no altitude test is reliable for a hovering mob without risking an advancement that never fires.
- **Medevac** — Function-granted by detect/ferry (mode 2) - the same detector file as Ferryman, because the two are the same machine: a vehicle, a second player aboard who has to keep meeting a condition, and a vehicle-distance statistic that has to advance while they do. The medium bucket calls the file once per mode, and each call is gated on the player missing that specific advancement, so each half's statistic mirror is refreshed on every cycle that half is used and neither can go stale behind the other. Mode 2 takes the happy_ghast_one_cm delta against the private bacaptmh_hgm mirror (seeded in seed.mcfunction, capped at 20000 cm per window), walks 'on vehicle ... on passengers' for a Happy Ghast, and requires a passenger other than the pilot whose bacaptmh_hp health score is 1..5 - alive and at two and a half hearts or less. A passenger who dies reads 0, fails the test and resets bacaptmh_medvac to 0, which is precisely the 'without them dying' clause; so does one who heals above the threshold, so the whole 200 blocks must be flown with them hurt. At 20000 cm everyone aboard is granted.
- **Scale Model** — Function-granted. bucket_slow (every 5 s) decays bacaptmh_sm, then stamps 120 - ten minutes of slow ticks - on every non-spectator who is in the Nether with another non-spectator at distance 0.1..5 (the lower bound excludes the player himself, whose distance to himself is always 0). detect/scale_model runs at each stamped player, returns unless the execution dimension is the Overworld, and grants to himself and to every stamped non-spectator at distance 800 or more; a distance argument makes the selector world-limited, so the far player is necessarily in the Overworld too. The pairing is by shared window rather than by identity: two players who each stood near somebody in the Nether within the same ten minutes will satisfy it even if they never stood near each other, and the stamp expires ten minutes after the Nether meeting.
- **Skull Session** — Pure criteria. 'summoned_it' copies BACAP technical/respawn_wither exactly - summoned_entity with an entity_type of wither, which fires only on the player who placed the last block of the summon. 'handed_a_skull' is the distance-min-1 thrower guard on a Wither Skeleton Skull. Reduced from the spare, and the reduction is the point: criteria are booleans, not counters, and three separate skull pickups from three distinct throwers would all latch the same single criterion on the first one, so 'three players each hand over a skull' cannot be expressed without a detector. Players drops from 4 to 2 and the tier from super_challenge to challenge to match what is actually being asked.
- **The Nether Express** — A per-player dimension score is seeded on first sight without arming anything, so a login is never read as a crossing. The dimension pass itself now runs unconditionally over @a at the top of the medium bucket, not inside the player-count and advancement gate that guards the co-location check: a mirror that only updates while a gate is open goes stale the moment the gate shuts, and when the gate reopened every player whose real dimension had drifted from their frozen score would arm a window in the same tick — four players who had been to the Nether and back while a fifth was offline could be granted a 55 XP goal by standing at spawn when that fifth logged in. It is also run for spectators, so a spectator who crosses does not come back to survival carrying a stale score. Any transition into or out of the Nether opens a twenty-medium-tick window, exactly ten seconds. The gated half then anchors on each windowed non-spectator and grants when four windowed non-spectators stand within 24 blocks — arrival points only land that close together if it was one portal — and zeroes their windows on the granting path so the counter cannot be reused. Known limit, stated plainly: dying in the Nether is also a dimension change, so four players wiping together and respawning at the same bed inside ten seconds would qualify. A PortalCooldown read would discriminate that, but its value for players is not something I could verify against this version's files, and a wrong guard there would make the advancement unobtainable, which is worse — so it is listed as a playtest item instead.
- **Through the Looking Glass** — Two minecraft:location criteria - the polling trigger BACAP uses for standing conditions, with its usual inverted spectator term - one per dimension, ANDed. Each requires the player's vehicle to be a #minecraft:boat (the entity tag BACAP itself uses) carrying a passenger that is a player at distance.absolute.min 0.3. Nested entity predicates are matched against the SAME origin vector as the outer one, i.e. the triggering player's own position, so that distance excludes the player themself (offset 0) and admits the other seat (vanilla seats a boat's two riders 0.2 and -0.6 along the hull, 0.8 apart). I deliberately did NOT hang this on changed_dimension with a vehicle predicate: I am not confident the rider is still attached to the boat at the instant that trigger fires, and a criterion that never fires is a dead advancement. The cost is honest - the pack verifies you shared the boat on both sides of the portal, not the transit itself.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Air Supply** — Pure criteria, one trigger, no function — the source half is Too Many Hands' own Field Medic structure and the fluid half is BACAP's from potion/marine_marauder. minecraft:effects_changed fires when Water Breathing lands; 'source' is the thrower of the splash potion, required to be a player at distance >= 1. That guard is exact here rather than approximate: if you splash yourself the source entity IS you, so the distance is 0 no matter how far the bottle flew, and it can never fire. The 'player' condition requires the recipient to be standing in water. Dropped from the idea: the 'last two air bubbles' and 'thirty blocks down' conditions — an entity predicate exposes neither air nor depth, so the description no longer claims them.
- **Bad Influence** — Pure criteria, no function, with the frame moved from the giver to the receiver so it can be expressed without one. 'received' is thrown_item_picked_up_by_player for an Ominous Bottle thrown by another player, carrying the distance absolute min 1 self-pickup guard, so a solo player cannot supply their own bottle. 'drank' is effects_changed on minecraft:bad_omen - the exact shape blazeandcave:adventure/hard_mode_activated uses for trial_omen, and in current versions Bad Omen has no source other than an Ominous Bottle. 'won' is minecraft:hero_of_the_village (attested in challenges/the_perfect_run). Default AND requirements. The three criteria are not linked in time, so strictly the bottle you were handed need not be the one that started the raid you won.
- **Caveat Emptor** — Pure criteria. The consume side copies BACAP potion/the_invisible_player and potion/true_feather_falling exactly - consume_item with an item predicate of minecraft:potion plus predicates.potion_contents, and both the base and strong variants, because BACAP always enumerates them. The supply side is the same two variants behind the distance-min-1 thrower guard. requirements is two AND groups, each an OR over the two potency variants, so all four criteria are covered exactly once: you must have been handed a Harming potion by another player, and you must have drunk one. Reframed from the spare's giver's-eye description: all criteria of an advancement fire on one player, so the pack can only pay the person who swallowed it, and the description is written from that player's side. Not time-linked, so a bottle taken today and drunk next week still counts.
- **Chemical Warfare** — The 'gassed' criterion fires on a victim only when ANOTHER player is the recorded source of a Poison effect, which rules out poisonous potatoes, spider eyes, suspicious stew, pufferfish and cave spiders in one stroke — those pass no player source. requirements [["gassed"],["impossible"]] cover the criteria set exactly. The fast bucket revokes the flag and then demands a lingering Poison cloud within 6 blocks, matched on the cloud's own contents (nbt={potion_contents:{potion:"minecraft:poison"}} plus strong_poison and long_poison), so Dragon's Breath and a Lingering Potion of Swiftness no longer qualify and a plain splash of Poison no longer qualifies either. A qualifying victim gets a four-second bacaptmh_gas window; three windowed victims within 12 blocks grants exactly those victims (gamemode=!spectator) plus whoever incremented minecraft.used:minecraft.lingering_potion in the last four seconds within 24 blocks. The old blanket 'grant everyone within 24 blocks' is gone, and the gas windows are zeroed on the granting path. This one has no equivalent of the Field Medic timing race, because an AreaEffectCloud waits 10 ticks before its first application, which is always at least one medium tick after the thrower's statistic rose.
- **Detox** — 'handed' is thrown_item_picked_up_by_player on a milk bucket with the thrower at distance.absolute.min 1, so the milk has to have come out of somebody else's hand. 'cured' is consume_item on a milk bucket with an effects predicate for minecraft:wither, copied from BACAP's Miracle Drink, which uses the identical shape for poison; the effect is read as the drink is consumed, before it is cleared. The two are ANDed but not linked in time, so the thirty-second window in the idea is not enforced.
- **Field Medic** — Rebuilt on a real trigger instead of a health delta. The 'dosed' criterion is minecraft:effects_changed with effects={minecraft:regeneration} and source = an entity_properties predicate pinning entity_type player plus distance absolute min 1 — the same self-exclusion idiom BACAP uses in mining/diamonds_to_you, in the source-field shape verified against Enhanced animal/buff_axolotl.json. Vanilla passes the potion's owner as the effect source, and a self-splash measures the distance between the patient and the source entity, which for one and the same entity is always 0, so it can never be self-served; beacons, golden apples and mob-applied poison pass no source at all and are excluded outright. requirements are [["dosed"],["impossible"]], which covers the criteria set exactly (so the file loads) and ANDs an impossible criterion in, so the flag alone never completes it. The fast bucket picks up players holding {dosed=true,impossible=false}, revokes the flag and reads Health once at scale 10 (Regeneration I heals no sooner than 50 ticks after application, so current health is still pre-potion health), requiring 1..59 — that is 0.1 to 5.9 HP, so a patient on a quarter heart is admitted where the old scale-1 read floored them to 0 and threw them out with the corpses, while a dead player's true 0 is still excluded and respawning still cannot fire it. The medic is then a different player within 16 blocks who either carries a live splash-potion window or still has an unconsumed minecraft.used:minecraft.splash_potion delta against its own mirror; accepting the pending delta as well as the window closes the race where a potion thrown at an ally two or three blocks away lands its effect 2-6 ticks after the statistic rises, before the next medium tick could turn that rise into a window. No health-snapshot objective is used any more, which also removes the bacaptmh_hp name collision with the A30 mob-health objective.
- **Fireproof Friendship** — The source half is lifted verbatim from this pack's own Field Medic (effects_changed with a source entity_properties condition pinning entity_type player and distance min 1 - a self-splash measures the distance from an entity to itself, always 0, so it can never be self-served) and the lava half from BACAP's Stayin' Frosty (location.fluid over lava and flowing_lava). effects_changed fires on an effect being added OR refreshed, so the natural play is: get Fire Resistance, get into the lava, and have your friend land a second Fire Resistance splash on you while you are in it. Deliberate change of subject: the criterion can only fire for the RECEIVING player, because no vanilla trigger reports a splash potion landing on somebody else, so the description now credits the swimmer rather than the thrower. Awarding the thrower instead would have cost a detection function for no gain in the moment being celebrated.
- **Group Buff** — Cost dropped from 112 NBT selector evaluations per slow tick to 8. One tag pass per buff effect (Speed, Strength, Regeneration, Fire Resistance, Water Breathing, Night Vision, Jump Boost, Invisibility) is the only time a player is serialised; from the tags it builds a count (bacaptmh_gb) and an eight-bit mask (bacaptmh_gbm, 1/2/4/.../128) with pure tag-selector score commands. It then anchors on each player carrying at least three effects and counts players within 16 blocks whose mask score is equal, which is a plain score comparison — no combinatorial trio walk at all. Four matching masks grants exactly those four. Requiring the mask to be identical rather than merely overlapping in three places is what makes the check cheap, and it is what a shared round of splash potions actually produces, so the description now says 'an identical set of three or more' rather than 'the same three'. The 16-block radius means four players in three dimensions no longer qualify.
- **Marco Polo** — minecraft:location fires for every player every 20 ticks, so the whole thing is a free once-a-second state check with no function. looking_at is a server-side raycast that ignores rendering entirely — it filters only spectators — so an invisible player is a perfectly valid hit even though the seeker cannot see them; that is the joke. The target must be a player carrying minecraft:invisibility (the effects predicate shape BACAP uses in The Ghastly Eyes) within 3 blocks. No self-exclusion guard is needed because a raycast from your own eyes can never return you. Spectators are excluded with the inverted gamemode term BACAP uses in Sky High and Silent But Deadly.
- **Milk Run** — Pure criteria, AND-ed, no detector and no bucket lines. Both halves land on the patient. `delivered` is the undying_fandom pickup shape with the giver pinned to a player at least a block away, so the bucket must have been thrown by somebody else - a self-drop is distance 0 and fails. `cured` is BACAP monsters/miracle_drink minus the health clause: consume_item on a milk bucket while the poison effect is active, which is attested to fire for milk because miracle_drink's #blazeandcave:poison_cures tag is consumed through this same trigger. The two acts are not time-linked; what makes this impossible alone is the handover, which no single player can satisfy.
- **Pharmacy Run** — Pure criteria, no detector. Three thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity criteria, one per potion, each requiring the picker to be a player at distance min 1 (so you cannot hand potions to yourself) and the item to carry the exact potion_contents component — the same `"components": {"minecraft:potion_contents": {"potion": ...}}` form BACAP uses for tipped arrows in adventure/undying_fandom. requirements AND the three groups, so all three potions must actually change hands. Honest limitations: the one-minute window and 'three DIFFERENT players' are not enforced — vanilla cannot count distinct recipients without a function — so the minimum player count is 2, not the 4 the idea suggested; and only vanilla-brewed potions with no custom colour or effects match the exact component.
- **Sharpshooter Surgeon** — The arrow's potion is read with the exact damage.type.direct_entity NBT match that ships in Enhanced Discoveries' bacaped:potion/healed_by_pain, mirrored onto player_hurt_entity so the shooter is the one credited rather than the patient. Both potency levels are separate criteria in one OR requirements group, which covers the criteria set exactly. player_hurt_entity has no self-exclusion of its own, so the victim carries the diamonds_to_you guard — entity_type player plus distance min 1 — which also rules out a ricochet landing back on the shooter. The health threshold from the original idea is gone: entity predicates expose no health field and NBT matching is exact-value only, so 'below two hearts' is not expressible without a detector, and this was not worth one. One assumption I am not able to test here: that player_hurt_entity fires on a hit whose net effect heals the target. Enhanced Discoveries proves the mirror-image entity_hurt_player does, because the arrow's impact damage is applied before the effect, so the same should hold — but it is the one thing in this batch I would want to see on a server before trusting.
- **Stop, Drop and Roll** — Detector bacaptmh:detect/stop_drop_and_roll, called unconditionally from bucket_medium so neither of its mirrors can freeze. It samples every non-spectator's Fire NBT into bacaptmh_fire each window and rolls the old value into bacaptmh_firep; a player whose previous sample still had 20 or more fire ticks left and who is not burning now had the fire cut short, because natural burn-down can only shed ten ticks between two samples ten ticks apart. Separately, any increase in the shared bacaptmh_splash (minecraft.used:minecraft.splash_potion) statistic against a private bacaptmh_sdrm mirror opens a 3-window flag on the thrower. When a doused player has a flagged thrower at distance 1..8 — never 0..8, so a self-splash can never pay out — both are granted. Known imprecision I am not hiding: the used-item statistic cannot tell a water bottle from any other splash potion, so a teammate throwing a healing potion at the exact moment you jumped into water would also satisfy it.
- **The Weakest Link** — Pure criteria, one criterion. player_killed_entity is native PvP - the first_blood shape already in this pack - and needs no self-exclusion guard because you cannot be the entity you killed. The victim's effects are read with the entity predicate's effects field in the same shape BACAP uses in enchanting/silent_but_deadly, so the kill only counts while Weakness is actually on them. What it cannot verify is who applied the Weakness or how: a victim who drank their own Potion of Weakness counts, and the splash is not required. The description says what the file actually checks rather than what the idea described.
- **Trauma Team** — One effects_changed criterion built on TMH potion/field_medic's exact source shape: the applying entity must be a player at distance min 1, so a self-splash (distance 0) can never satisfy it. The five effects are checked against the whole active set at that instant, and Resistance can only arrive from a Potion of the Turtle Master, so the team really has to stack five separate splashes on one person. Mechanism changed from the idea and this is the reason: Absorption is not a brewable potion and Instant Health never becomes an active effect, so the stated 'Regeneration + Instant Health + Absorption' combination cannot be expressed by any trigger. Criteria also cannot prove the throwers were three distinct people, so the honest minimum is 2.
- **Triage** — One detector (detect/m1_triage), granted by function. Instant Health is applied without ever being added as an effect instance, so effects_changed - the trigger Field Medic uses for Regeneration - does not fire for it; this is measured instead. minecraft.used:minecraft.splash_potion is compared against its own private mirror (seeded in seed_lines, refreshed ungated) and a rise opens a 2-medium-tick window on the thrower. A health objective, bacaptmh_m1hp, is differenced against bacaptmh_m1hpm, which is only advanced AFTER the detector has run, so bacaptmh_m1hpm is the health each player had before this window and bacaptmh_m1hpd is the change. The detector runs at the thrower, tags him out of the count so he cannot heal himself into the total, then counts players within 8 blocks whose health rose this window from 5 or less - three hearts - and grants to the thrower and all of them at three. That is four distinct players. Honest limits: the potion's contents are not read, so any splash that raises three low players' health in the same half second qualifies, and the medium cadence means the before/after pair spans ten ticks rather than a single instant.

</details>
