| ◆ | **Special Delivery** | Redstone | Drop a parcel on the line and have a teammate scoop it up from a rolling Minecart eight blocks away (2 players) | 55 |
| · | **Split the Signal** | Redstone | Craft a Comparator out of Nether Quartz another player mined (2 players) | 15 |
| ◆ | **The Great Train Robbery** | Redstone | Kill another player while they are riding in a Minecart (2 players) | 50 |
| · | **Toll Booth** | Redstone | Put up an Iron Door and charge another player sixteen Emeralds for passage (2 players) | 30 |
| · | **Trapped!** | Redstone | Have another player set off a trapped chest that you just placed (2 players) | 20 |
| ◆ | **Trolley Problem** | Redstone | Shoot another player dead from your Minecart while they ride a Minecart fifteen blocks down the line (2 players) | 40 |
| ◆ | **Vending Machine** | Redstone | Have another player feed an Emerald into your Hopper and take what your Dispenser gives back (2 players) | 350 |

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Assembly Line** — Four ANDed criteria: receive a stack of at least five iron ingots from another player, receive a chest from another player, craft minecraft:hopper (recipe_crafted, BACAP's standard form), then hand the finished hopper on to a third pair of hands via thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity. count {min:5} is the range form BACAP already uses. Criteria cannot compare the two donors' identities, so the enforced minimum is 2 players even though the intended shape is three; the 1200-tick window from the idea is likewise not expressible and has been dropped rather than faked. No function.
- **Bumper Carts** — Function-granted. detect/bumper_carts confirms the player is riding a minecart with 'execute as @s on vehicle if entity @s[type=minecart]', then looks for the collision signature: bacaptmh_bcprev (last window's minecart_one_cm delta) at 400 cm or more — roughly full speed — and bacaptmh_cartd (this window's) at 20 or less, i.e. stopped dead inside ten ticks, with a second non-spectator, non-creative player 0.5 to 3.5 blocks away showing exactly the same pair. Both deltas come from detect/sample, which is ungated and self-seeding. bcprev is refreshed by an UNGATED bucket line that runs AFTER the detector, deliberately: refreshing it inside the function would mean whichever rider the bucket processed first had already overwritten their own previous value, and the collision would only ever be visible from one side. Honest approximation: nothing here reads Motion, so 'head-on' and 'knocked backwards' are inferred from two fast carts stopping together rather than measured. Two riders in the SAME cart are excluded by distance=0.5..; a shared station stop is excluded by needing both to have been at full speed the previous window.
- **Conveyor Belt** — One criterion, structured exactly like BACAP nether/im_in_lava_with_you.json: a condition list on the other entity and a matching one on 'player', each an entity_properties term with the fluid predicate, water and flowing_water both listed the way potion/marine_marauder.json lists them. The pickup itself is thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity, which resolves the thrower by UUID and fires no matter how far away they are, so distance min 1 becomes distance min 50 and does the two-player work by itself - you cannot be fifty blocks from yourself. Both ends standing in water is the proxy for 'there was a channel'; the criterion cannot watch the item's path, so a fifty-block gap and wet feet at both ends is all it really proves. That is why the spare's fast-bucket path-sampling function was dropped rather than reduced.
- **Convoy** — detect/sample turns each player's minecart_one_cm statistic into a per-window delta. It runs ungated for every non-spectator and seeds the baseline the first time it sees a player, so a parked player can never be credited with their lifetime minecart distance and the baseline cannot go stale when the advancement gate flips. The check then tags every distinct minecraft:minecart within 20 blocks that is carrying a non-spectator, non-creative player with a positive delta, counts the tagged carts, and grants to their passengers at four. Counting carts rather than riders is what stops four players in one cart from qualifying. 'The same track' is approximated by the 20-block radius rather than by a rail block test, because no rails block tag is attested in the installed packs.
- **Mind the Gap** — placed_block with a location_check on the four rail blocks, in the shape TMH's own falling_out uses, plus an impossible guard in its own requirements group. detect/mind_the_gap runs each medium tick for holders of the latch (gamemode=!spectator and !creative, since this measures block placement), revokes it, and counts players inside 1..16 blocks whose minecart_one_cm delta over that same half-second is positive — a private mirror, bacaptmh_mc/_mcp/_mcd, seeded in bacaptmh:seed so a lifetime cart total can never read as a fresh event. If anybody qualifies, both the rail-layer and every rolling rider are granted. What is verified is 'a rail went down within 16 blocks of a moving cart in the same half-second', not that the cart was aimed at that exact block; the sub-second timing in the original wording is beyond what a 10-tick bucket can resolve.
- **Rail Retrieval** — SWAPPED IN for 'Nautilus Ambulance', which I could not make safe at 0 detectors for two separate reasons. There is no air-supply field in any entity predicate, so 'air nearly gone' is unreadable; and a shared Nautilus is unverified -- both existing Nautilus advancements in this pack, bacaptmh:biomes/submarine_crew and the authored biomes/the_cousteau_manoeuvre, put one player on each Nautilus, which points at a single-rider mount. I was not willing to ship a vehicle.passenger check on a mob I cannot confirm seats two. Rail Retrieval is a spare and both halves are checkable. 'laid_the_line' is placed_block with a location_check on dimension minecraft:the_nether and block.blocks listing the four rails explicitly (rail, powered_rail, detector_rail, activator_rail -- written out rather than as the #minecraft:rails tag, because neither installed pack references that tag and the verifier's registry check is happier with concrete block IDs). 'the_delivery' is thrown_item_picked_up_by_player on minecraft:chest_minecart, thrower guarded as entity_type minecraft:player at distance.absolute.min 1, with the receiving player's own location also pinned to the Nether. Both required, so it is impossible alone. WHAT IS FICTION, said plainly: the original wanted the cart to actually travel 100+ blocks down a track and be opened by a player carrying no armour and under five items. None of that is expressible -- a chest minecart in item form carries no contents, there is no odometer, and inventory-emptiness is not a predicate. What the pack really checks is 'you built rail in the Nether' and 'another player handed you a Chest Minecart while you were down there'. The description above is worded to promise only that. Parent is redstone/moving_storage (Put a chest in a minecart) so it lands next to the cart it is about; redstone/railwayman was the alternative if a reviewer prefers the rail-laying emphasis.
- **Signal Boost** — Detection function on the medium bucket, run for every non-spectator, non-creative player and never gated on who already holds it, so a holder can still be somebody's partner. Each player's used:repeater delta feeds a per-player tally whose 1200-tick window opens on the first placement of a run and is not extended by later ones; when two players within 32 blocks both stand at 30 or more inside their windows, both are granted. Tracing one connected redstone line and a pulse from end to end was not implementable inside the budget, so the advancement is the co-op build - thirty each, together, in a minute - rather than the circuit.
- **Special Delivery** — One criterion. The pickup is diamonds_to_you's trigger with the receiver constrained three ways: entity_type minecraft:player, distance min 8 from the sender at the moment of pickup (which also self-excludes, since you are never eight blocks from yourself), and a vehicle predicate of entity_type minecraft:minecart - the same vehicle field BACAP monsters/ride_or_dry.json uses on its victims. A player cannot ride a Chest Minecart, so the rideable cart is the one that can carry a courier, and the item has to be lying on the line for them to sweep up as they pass. The spare's version - recording a Chest Minecart's contents and position when the loader last closed it, then matching the opener a hundred blocks later - is a stateful function; this keeps the freight-by-rail act and spends nothing. It does not check that a rail was involved, only that the receiver was in a minecart.
- **Toll Booth** — Pure criteria, no detector. 'booth' copies blazeandcave:redstone/target_acquired's placed_block shape exactly — a location list holding one minecraft:location_check with a block predicate — swapping target for iron_door. 'toll' is the guarded pickup with an item count bound of min 16, the same MinMaxBounds field bacaped:challenges/so_bright uses on an ItemPredicate; the sixteen emeralds must arrive as one stack thrown by another player. Documented reframe: the idea wanted the payer to then walk through the door inside a 200-tick window, which needs a detector and a placement log. That half is roleplay here — the advancement verifies that you built the door and that somebody paid the toll, not that they passed through it.
- **Trapped!** — Opening a trapped chest latches the `opened` criterion, using the same default_block_use + location_check shape BACAP uses in redstone/troll_chest. The detector consumes the criterion, then grants to the opener and to any other player within 32 blocks whose bacaptmh_tct window is still live, i.e. who placed a trapped chest in the last minute.
- **Vending Machine** — Hybrid. The two real criteria exist only to identify a shopkeeper - a player who has placed both a Hopper and a Dispenser - and the impossible guard sits in its OWN requirements group, so the three groups are ANDed and the advancement can never complete on criteria alone. The detector runs in bucket_fast but is reached only through two cheap gates: a player standing directly on a Hopper, or a player inside the ten-second window that paying opens. It opens that window when an emerald item entity is within two blocks of a customer stood on a Hopper (a dropped emerald falls onto the Hopper and is sucked in), then looks for an item within six blocks lying against a Dispenser on any of its six faces or inside its block space, with no Thrower tag and not an emerald - a thrown item always carries Thrower, a dispensed one never does, which is what stops a hand-dropped decoy. It grants only when a DIFFERENT player (distance=1..32, which excludes the customer at distance 0) holds both build criteria, so nobody can vend to themselves, and it grants shopkeeper and customer together.

</details>

## Statistics — Too Many Hands

| | Advancement | Tab | What you do | XP |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| ★ | **Antipodes** | Statistics | Have two players stand on exactly mirrored coordinates, at least three thousand blocks from the world origin (2 players) | 200 |
| ◆ | **Blimpin' Ain't Easy** | Statistics | Fly 5 km on a Happy Ghast without landing, with at least one passenger aboard the whole time (2 players) | 70 |
| ★ | **Blood Feud** | Statistics | Kill another player and be killed by another player in every dimension (2 players) | 120 |
| ◆ | **Body Count** | Statistics | Have five players who have each died at least ten times all be online together (5 players) | 60 |
| ★ | **Bread Winner** | Statistics | Hand food to other players five hundred times (2 players) | 200 |
| ◆ | **Crash Test Dummy** | Statistics | Take five hundred points of damage from other players (2 players) | 75 |
| ★ | **Gift Economy** | Statistics | Hand items to other players five hundred times (2 players) | 250 |
| ★ | **Heart Surgeon** | Statistics | Supply other players with a hundred hearts of medicine: Golden Apples, Golden Carrots, Steak, and Splash Potions of Healing and Regeneration (2 players) | 150 |
| ◆ | **Long Distance Relationship** | Statistics | Have two players at least 10,000 blocks apart in the same dimension (2 players) | 50 |
| ◆ | **Philanthropist** | Statistics | Give away a full stack of sixty-four Diamonds in one go (2 players) | 250 |
| ◆ | **Serial Offender** | Statistics | Kill fifty other players (2 players) | 40 |
| ★ | **Sparring Partners** | Statistics | Trade a hundred hits with another player without either of you dying (2 players) | 150 |
| ★ | **The Amazing Race** | Statistics | Leave one spot with another player, both cover 5,000 blocks, and both be back inside a minute of each other (2 players) | 500 |
| ★ | **Trading Floor** | Statistics | Rack up a thousand Villager trades between two or more players (2 players) | 200 |

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Antipodes** — Function-granted (detectors 2 and 3 of 3), on the 100-tick slow bucket. For each player who lacks it, detect/antipodes tags itself, stores its Pos[0] and Pos[2] as integers, and runs detect/antipodes_pair as every non-spectator at distance 6000 or more. That selector range is the radius test and the dimension test in one: mirroring about 0,0 at radius r puts the pair exactly 2r apart, so 6000 blocks of separation IS 3000 blocks of radius each, and a distance= selector only ever matches entities in the executing dimension, so a pair split across the Nether cannot qualify. The pair function adds the two x's and the two z's and grants when both sums land inside a 3-block tolerance — which covers the truncation in `data get ... 1` on each reading plus a block of slack for standing exactly on the mark. Both halves of the pair are granted: @s and the tagged outer player, so nobody is left out with Cooperative Mode off. The tag is removed at the end of the outer function and cleared again in start_extra. No unbounded selector: every @a here carries either a distance range or a tag plus limit=1.
- **Blimpin' Ain't Easy** — One detector (detect/m1_blimp), granted by function. minecraft.custom:minecraft.happy_ghast_one_cm - the same statistic BACAP statistics/big_blimp_enthusiast and test_flight read - is turned into a per-window delta against its own private mirror, which is seeded in seed_lines before any detector runs and refreshed UNGATED in the bucket, so a player who has ridden a ghast before this pack was installed can never have that lifetime total banked as one flight. The detector runs every medium tick for every non-spectator, non-creative player who lacks it: it walks `on vehicle ... on passengers` and sets an ok flag only if the vehicle is a Happy Ghast with OnGround:0b that also carries a second player at distance 0.5 or more (the rider is 0.0 from himself, so he cannot count as his own passenger). Any failure - dismounting, the passenger leaving, the ghast settling on the ground - zeroes the tally, which is what 'without landing' means here. Otherwise the delta is added and, at 500000 cm, every player passenger of that ghast is granted, not just the one who happened to trip the check. Cadence caveat: the bucket samples every ten ticks, so up to half a second of travel either side of a passenger leaving is counted before the reset.
- **Bread Winner** — Criteria latch plus one counting detector. 'fed' fires when another player picks up food you threw; the distance absolute min 1 guard means picking your own food back up never counts, and an explicit item list is used because no #minecraft:foods tag is attested in the reference packs. requirements are [["fed"],["impossible"]] - an AND of two groups, not one group containing both, so the latch alone can never complete the advancement and only the function can grant it. Every medium tick, any player holding {fed=true,impossible=false} runs detect/bread_winner, which revokes the criterion FIRST (so a failed pass can never leave it latched and re-fire), adds one to bacaptmh_fed and grants at 500. One latched half-second window is one tick of the tally, so a whole stack thrown and collected in one go counts once - hence the description says 'hand food over five hundred times', not 'five hundred individual items'. The tally is created with 'add 0' in the seed, following the Barn Raising convention, so a /reload cannot wipe a run in progress.
- **Crash Test Dummy** — Attribution is done by the criterion, not by proximity guesswork: 'hurt' arms only when the damage source_entity is a player at distance min 1, which is the BACAP diamonds_to_you idiom and is exactly the self-exclusion the brief warns about - your own arrow falling back on you has source_entity = you at distance 0 and cannot arm it. The damage_taken mirror is seeded in seed_lines and its per-window delta is computed ungated for everyone in the medium bucket, before any gate, so a quiet hour of mob damage can never be banked and then dumped into the tally by one punch. detect/crash_test_dummy releases the flag and adds only that window's delta to bacaptmh_ptd, granting at 5000 (the damage statistic is stored at ten units per damage point, so 5000 units is 500 points). Known slack: any non-player damage taken in the same half-second window as a player hit is counted too.
- **Gift Economy** — The `gave` criterion is an ordinary thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity with the one-block self-exclusion; the impossible guard keeps criteria alone from ever completing it, and requirements is an AND of two single-criterion groups so the guard is not OR'd away. A one-line detector on the fast bucket adds 1 and revokes `gave` again, which makes the score an exact count of real hand-offs rather than a statistic delta - minecraft.custom:minecraft.drop counts drop presses whether or not anybody picked them up. At most one is counted per tick, so an item shower into a crowd is undercounted; the original 'five hundred items' is therefore worded as five hundred hand-offs.
- **Heart Surgeon** — Five ANDed thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity criteria — the donor-side trigger, so every one of them is credited to the supplier and every one of them requires a different player to do the picking up (entity_type player, distance min 1 rejects picking your own drop back up). Stack sizes use the count {min:N} range form; the potion criteria use the minecraft:potion_contents item predicate BACAP already uses for harming and turtle_master. Mechanism substituted openly: no statistic anywhere counts health restored on another player, so an accumulator was impossible without a detector; this measures the supply side instead and keeps the 'hundred hearts' framing.
- **Long Distance Relationship** — The slow bucket anchors on each non-spectator lacking the advancement and tests @a[gamemode=!spectator,distance=10000..]; a distance argument makes the selector world-limited, so a match is automatically two players in the same dimension. Both selectors and the bucket guard now carry gamemode=!spectator, so a lone player plus a spectator ghosting 10 km away no longer completes it and a spectator is never granted. Anchoring on the player who lacks it still grants both ends, so a veteran at the far end is not a lockout.
- **Philanthropist** — Pure criteria, no scoreboard, no detector — the count is expressed as the stack size rather than as a running tally, which is what removes the function the mechanism note assumed. minecraft:thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity fires on the THROWER when something else picks their drop up, so 'entity' is the recipient: required to be entity_type minecraft:player at distance >= 1 from the giver, which excludes picking your own stack back up (that resolves at distance 0). item.count min 64 means the whole stack has to leave your hands as one item entity, so it cannot be dribbled out a few at a time. Earned by the giver only.
- **Serial Offender** — No detector function at all — one threshold line in bucket_slow grants it to any non-spectator whose bacaptmh_pk50 score reaches 50. bacaptmh_pk50 is a fresh objective on the vanilla playerKillCount criterion; it deliberately does NOT reuse the pack's existing bacaptmh_pkill, because f2_init zeroes that one for every player and start_extra makes f2_init re-run on every reload, which would silently reset a fifty-kill career each time the datapack was reloaded. Nothing is seeded and nothing is consumed: it is a lifetime threshold, not a delta, so there is no window in which a stale mirror could be misread.
- **Sparring Partners** — Both halves are native PvP criteria - player_hurt_entity for the blow dealt and entity_hurt_player with damage.source_entity for the blow taken, the latter being BACAP monsters/blast_shield's shape - and both carry the distance min 1 guard, without which your own arrow falling back on you counts as an opponent. requirements are [[hit],[hurt],[impossible]], three AND groups, so the criteria alone never complete it. bucket_fast increments bacaptmh_sp_h and bacaptmh_sp_t once per tick per flag and revokes each flag in the same tick, so nothing latches; two hits inside one tick are counted once. bucket_medium wipes both counters for anyone whose bacaptmh_sp_d deathCount rose, and then consumes that counter, so a counter that is consumed rather than compared cannot produce a first-run false positive. detect/spar grants only when TWO players within 24 blocks have both reached 100 given and 100 taken - that mutual threshold is what stands in for 'the same player', which no scoreboard can pin, and it is also what enforces 'without either of you dying', since either death resets one side of the pair.
- **The Amazing Race** — Detector, bucket_slow (every five seconds). Standing within three blocks of another player re-plants your start line at your feet, but only while your run's best is still under 5,000 blocks, so meeting again at the finish cannot wipe the run. Each cycle the squared horizontal distance from the start line is computed - both axis deltas clamped to 6,000 before squaring, so the multiply cannot overflow a 32-bit score - and the best is kept. Coming back within three blocks of the start line after a best of 5,000 or more stamps a finish time, and two finishers standing together whose stamps are inside 1,200 ticks of each other are both granted. Nether coordinates are not a shortcut, because the check is on raw coordinates against an overworld start line. The idea's 'in opposite directions' clause is dropped: a per-pair bearing test was neither cheap nor reliable, so what is enforced is the round trip and the shared finish, not the heading.
- **Trading Floor** — Function-granted. detect/trading_floor runs in the slow bucket UNGATED for every non-spectator, exactly like detect/sample: if it only ran while somebody still lacked the advancement the mirror would freeze the moment the last holder earned it and the next delta read would be a lifetime total. It takes each player's minecraft.custom:minecraft.traded_with_villager delta against a private mirror seeded in seed.mcfunction, adds it to that player's own bacaptmh_tradec contribution and to the shared #pool bacaptmh_tf, with the per-window delta bounded to 1..1000 so a mirror that somehow went stale cannot dump a lifetime total into the pool. The grant lines live in bucket_slow, not in a detector: contributors of 100 or more are counted into #contrib, and at a pool of 1000 with two or more such contributors every player with a non-zero contribution is granted. The pool is cumulative from pack install and never resets; the per-player floor of 100 is what makes it impossible for one trader to carry the whole thousand.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **All For One** — The slow bucket tags every non-spectator, then strips the tag off anyone failing 'if items entity @s armor.head/chest/legs/feet' for the four Netherite pieces, and off anyone with no Elytra in hotbar.*, inventory.* or weapon.offhand. Both the qualifying count and the online count are built by counting entities into a score, never by an @a[limit=N] test. If the two are equal and at least three non-spectators are online, all of them are granted. Both scratch tags are cleared at both ends.
- **Fashion Police** — Reuses the same live trim index Dress Code computes. Around each trimmed player the detector tags exactly one wearer per trim material - the same one-slot-per-category trick Split the Party uses for dimensions - and grants only to the tagged five or more once five different materials are present within 32 blocks. Crediting every trimmed player in range would have handed the challenge to seven people wearing five materials between them, which is not what the description says. A single player can only ever hold one index, because the index is only set when all four pieces carry the same material, so five tags means five people. Nothing depends on advancement criteria, so a player who already owns it still supplies their colour to the line-up instead of being invisible to the count.
- **Grand Central** — Function-granted. detect/grand_central reuses Convoy's distinct-vehicle pattern exactly: every non-spectator, non-creative player within 16 blocks whose minecart_one_cm delta is positive (so they are actually rolling) and whose minecraft.used:minecraft.rail total is at least 200 is stepped onto their vehicle, each minecraft:minecart so found is tagged, and the TAGGED CARTS are counted - four riders stacked in one cart cannot pass for four lines. At four distinct carts every passenger of them is granted. The cart delta comes from the pack's existing detect/sample, which runs ungated and seeds its baseline on first sight. The rail count is a deliberate lifetime total, not a delta, so no seeding is involved. What is approximated: the '16-block hub' stands in for a built junction, and only plain Rails count toward the two hundred (powered, detector and activator rails are separate used-statistics and are not summed), so the requirement is two hundred plain Rails each.
- **Insurance Policy** — Vanilla awards minecraft.used:minecraft.totem_of_undying the moment a totem saves you; the delta against its own private mirror opens a ten-medium-tick window, exactly five seconds rather than 5.5. The check anchors on a totem user and counts windowed non-spectators within 48 blocks, so it is now one shared disaster rather than four unrelated fights that happened to line up in time. Four people simultaneously one hit from death in the same place is the whole point.
- **Nobody Dies Tonight** — Detector bacaptmh:detect/nobody_dies_tonight, called unconditionally from bucket_slow so the death counter is consumed even when the streak is not running. One shared fake-player counter #streak on bacaptmh_ndt gains 100 per slow tick while #players (counted by f4_count, never by a limit= selector) is 3 or more, and is set to 0 by any player carrying a bacaptmh_ndtd deathCount of 1 or more or by the headcount falling below three. deathCount is zeroed for @a every slow tick so a single death can only break the streak once, and each player's counter is zeroed the first time bacaptmh:seed sees them, so a lifetime death total is never read as a fresh death. At 168000 accumulated ticks the advancement is granted to every non-spectator online and the counter restarts.
- **The Gauntlet** — requirements are [["hand_off"],["impossible"]], covering the criteria set exactly so the file loads, while still being uncompletable without a command grant. The direction of the trigger is confirmed from BACAP's own husbandry/allay_deliver_item_to_player and adventure/awards_ceremony, both of which award the RECEIVER and describe 'this' in the entity predicate as the thrower (there, an allay). The self-hand-off hole is therefore closed with BACAP's own guard from mining/diamonds_to_you: distance absolute min 1 on the thrower predicate, which for a player collecting their own dropped box measures the distance from an entity to itself and is always 0. Five players each re-collecting their own box no longer completes a 500 XP super challenge. The fast bucket revokes the flag, marks the leg's dimension, tags the receiver if they are new to this run and bumps a global chain counter; five distinct receivers plus all three dimension flags grants everyone tagged. Because only receivers are counted, a straight five-player line produces four receivers — the relay has to come back round to the player who started it, which a ring of five does naturally and which is what 'through five different players' means here. A thirty-minute idle timer, decremented in the slow bucket, wipes the chain and stands in for 'one Shulker Box' — the item's identity itself is not tracked. Known limit, same as Barn Raising: a player who already holds this cannot re-arm the criterion, so a later relay needs five players who all still lack it.
- **The Relay** — Detector, one file. Boss kills are read from the minecraft.killed:minecraft.wither / .warden / .ender_dragon statistics against private mirrors, so no entity iteration and no Health polling is needed. detect/relay runs as/at every non-spectator on a medium tick while three or more are online and somebody still lacks it; a boss flag is set only when the killer's statistic rose in that window AND some OTHER player's death stamp (bacaptmh_dtime, maintained ungated by the pack's existing detect/sample) is under 1200 ticks old — the killer is tagged out of that count, so dying to your own Wither and then killing it yourself proves nothing. When all three global flags stand, every non-spectator within 128 blocks of the finisher is granted. Deliberately weakened from the idea: 'three distinct finishers' and 'one session' are not tracked; the three flags are global and permanent, and the requirement is simply that a teammate lay dead in each fight.
- **Warden Party** — Same shape as Wither Committee but on the Warden at 16 blocks. 'Nobody dead' is enforced with the pack's death mirror rather than with a snapshot of the death screen: bacaptmh_deaths is a vanilla deathCount objective, sampled and consumed every window in detect/sample, which stamps bacaptmh_dtime with the gametime of the player's last death (the Health:0.0f test is kept as a second path). A counted player must be within 16 blocks, not on the death screen, and must have no death stamp inside the last 1200 ticks, so dying at any point in the last minute of the fight disqualifies you for the rest of it. That is enforceable on a 10-tick sampler and works with doImmediateRespawn on, unlike the old 'is on the death screen at this exact sample' test, which a player who died and respawned walked straight through. The Warden's death animation keeps the entity around long enough for the sampler to catch Health 0.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Ashes to Ashes** — Detector, one file. Every medium tick, while at least one player still lacks it, detect/ashes_to_ashes runs as/at each survival player: their Y is read only when the execution dimension is the End (hence the bucket line's 'at @s'), and below Y=-32 there is nothing left to stand on, so they are already falling. That stamps a 20-window (20 medium ticks = 200 ticks = ten seconds). The function then counts open windows and grants to every player holding one as soon as there are two. It fires on the plunge rather than on the death itself, which is why no death-cause plumbing is needed.
- **Congregation** — The slow bucket counts non-spectator players who are NOT in minecraft:the_end by iterating and adding, never by an @a[limit=N] test. If that count is zero and at least three non-spectators are online, everyone non-spectator is granted. Spectators are ignored so an AFK observer cannot block it, and the three-player floor stops it firing for a lone player wandering the End. Nobody is excluded from the count for already holding it, so a veteran in the End does not break it for the rest.
- **Eye Contact** — The criterion is copied straight off BACAP's Wax Off: item_used_on_block with a location_check on the block and a match_tool on the item, here end_portal_frame and ender_eye. detect/eye_contact adds one to a lifetime bacaptmh_eye tally, stamps a 30-second bacaptmh_eyt freshness window, releases the criterion so the next eye re-arms it, then counts non-spectators within 12 blocks who have seated at least six eyes AND are still inside their freshness window; at two or more it grants to all of them. The eye tally is a counter this pack increments itself, not a mirrored statistic, so there is no lifetime-total-read-as-an-event hazard. The honest limitation: the tally is lifetime rather than per-portal, so the six-each threshold can be reached across two strongholds - deliberately, because a stronghold portal can spawn with up to three eyes already seated and a strict per-portal six-each would then be unreachable. The freshness window plus the 12-block radius is what makes it 'this portal, together, now'.
- **Formation Flying** — BACAP's own blazeandcave:is_flying predicate (flags.is_flying, i.e. Elytra gliding) finds the gliders; their Y goes into a score and those at Y>=200 are tagged. Each tagged glider is an anchor: with three tagged gliders within 10 blocks a persistence counter goes up, and the moment the condition fails it is reset to zero. The grant only happens at four consecutive medium ticks, so the formation has to hold for two full seconds — the old version was a single instantaneous sample that three players diving through the same airspace could satisfy in one frame, which is what the old note wrongly claimed it prevented. Counters are zeroed on the granting path and for anyone not currently in formation.
- **Ghost Protocol** — Function-granted. Same dragon sampler as detect/tag_team but on its own private stamp objective (bacaptmh_gpt), so it does not go dark once everybody holds Tag Team and that detector stops being dispatched. Every 0.5 s the dragon's Health NBT is read; when it drops, every non-spectator non-creative player within 128 blocks whose damage_dealt delta is positive in the same window is stamped with the gametime. The delta comes from the pack's existing ungated, self-seeding detect/sample. At Health 0 (the dragon lingers through its ~200-tick death animation) the wounders stamped in the last 2400 ticks are counted, and separately the wounders who ALSO carry a death stamp under 2400 ticks old are counted; the advancement is granted to every wounder only when the two counts are equal and there are at least four. 'Died during the fight' is therefore approximated as 'died in the last two minutes', and attribution is by damage-statistic delta plus proximity rather than by a real damage-source hook, exactly as Tag Team already is.
- **Heir Apparent** — One requirements group holding both criteria, so this is deliberately an OR: the player who throws the egg earns it through 'gave' (thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity, the trigger that fires for the thrower, with the picker-up as 'entity') and the player who picks it up earns it through 'got' (thrown_item_picked_up_by_player, which fires for the receiver with the thrower as 'entity'). Both carry entity_type player and distance.absolute.min 1, so dropping the egg and picking it back up yourself is excluded on both sides. There is exactly one Dragon Egg per world, so the handover is the whole ceremony.
- **Into the Void** — Pure criteria, no detector. player_hurt_entity with entity_type player is native PvP; the distance min 1 guard is mandatory here because this trigger has no self-exclusion and an arrow that comes back down on its own shooter would otherwise satisfy it. The location predicate on the hurt player - dimension the_end plus position.y at most 20 - is the Enhanced Discoveries challenges/what_are_the_chances shape (entity.location.position.y range) and is what makes it 'into the void': End terrain sits well above Y=20, so a player there is already falling, and void damage does not start until Y=-64, leaving roughly two and a half seconds to land the shot. The damage predicate nests tags and direct_entity under type exactly as BACAP adventure/picador does. The Punch enchantment itself is not checked - no damage predicate carries the bow - so the description states the intent rather than a verified condition. Reworded from 'knock a player into the void': out_of_world deaths carry no killer, so nothing can attribute the kill.
- **Pearl Swap** — DETECTOR 2 of 5, function-granted (impossible criterion). The minecraft.used:minecraft.ender_pearl mirror is consumed UNGATED in bucket_fast — three commands for @a every tick, never behind the advancement gate — so it can never go stale and read a lifetime throw count as a fresh throw; seed_lines and start_extra both prime bacaptmh_pearlp. A throw opens a 100-tick window (bacaptmh_pw) and records the launch block in bacaptmh_px/py/pz via `data get entity @s Pos[n] 1`. While a window is live, detect/pearl_pair copies A's launch point and current position into shared #-scores, requires A to have moved more than 7 blocks in x or z (otherwise two players throwing pearls at their own feet side by side would pass every test below), and walks every OTHER player with a live window into detect/pearl_check, which grants both when B is within 3 blocks of A's launch point AND A is within 3 blocks of B's. Honest limitations: coordinates are integer-truncated, so tolerance near x=0 or z=0 is off by one block; and the check is on positions, not on the teleports themselves, so two players who threw pearls and then simply walked into each other's launch spots inside five seconds would also qualify.
- **Rocket Man** — minecraft:thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity on a Firework Rocket where both the thrower and the picker carry flags.is_flying with an Elytra in the chest slot - the elytra-gliding test BACAP uses in technical/divers_dozen_count and Enhanced uses in nether/flap_dont_fall, not creative flight. The one-block floor on the picker keeps a rocket you drop and fly back into from counting. Note the title sits very close to BACAP's own end/rocketman, which is also this advancement's parent.
- **Tag Team** — Every 0.5 s while an Ender Dragon exists in the End the detector samples the dragon's Health NBT; when it drops, every non-spectator, non-creative player within 128 blocks whose damage_dealt delta for that same window is positive gets a wounder stamp (bacaptmh_dgt = gametime). The delta comes from detect/sample, which runs UNGATED for every non-spectator every window and seeds its baseline the first time it sees a player, so a player who has never been sampled can never have their lifetime damage_dealt read as a fresh hit. When the dragon's Health reaches 0 (it lingers through the ~200-tick death animation, so there are many sampling chances) the advancement is granted to every player within 256 blocks whose stamp is under 2400 ticks old, provided there are at least two of them. Attribution is by damage-statistic delta plus proximity rather than a real damage-source hook, so hitting some other mob in the same half second the dragon is wounded could in principle tag a bystander; the two-minute stamp window means a player who stopped fighting long before the kill stops counting.
- **The Ferryman Cometh** — Detector bacaptmh:detect/ferryman, dispatched from bucket_slow as/at every non-spectator who still lacks it, gated on 2 or more players online and on 'if dimension minecraft:the_end'. The function walks 'on vehicle ... on passengers' exactly as detect/carpool does (players are never written into a vehicle's Passengers NBT) and requires the vehicle to be a happy_ghast carrying at least two players; dismounting or losing the passenger sets the flag back to 0 and returns. Progress is then a three-stage flag driven by nested box selectors on @s, which are world-limited to the execution dimension: stage 1 inside a 192-block box over the centre, stage 2 in the open band between 600 and 900 blocks out, and the grant when they pass 1000 blocks out. An End Gateway drops a player from the centre straight to roughly a thousand blocks, so it can never touch the 600-900 band — that band is what makes the Gateway route unrewardable. The grant fires 'on vehicle on passengers' so every player aboard is credited, not just the one the loop happened to be running as.
- **Up and Away** — No detector. Each player's own effects_changed criterion latches the moment Levitation lands; the medium bucket turns that latch into a 20-medium-tick window - exactly the ten seconds a Shulker bullet grants - and revokes the latch again on the same pass, so the window is a live event and never a permanent flag. effects_changed re-fires on re-application, so a second bullet refreshes the window instead of letting it lapse mid-fight. The bucket then counts non-spectators holding a live window with `execute store result ... run execute if entity` (never a limit= selector) and grants to all of them at three. Levitation has no potion in vanilla, so in survival the only source is a Shulker bullet; that is why the source is not separately verified, and it is stated here rather than hidden. Counting is global rather than within a radius because a proximity test would need an `at @s` anchor and therefore a function; in practice three players levitating in the same ten seconds are in the same End city.
- **Wingman** — Pure criteria, AND-ed by default. 'gift' is an elytra picked up from another player's drop, with the thrower at distance >= 1 so self-drops are out. 'took_off' copies BACAP's own gliding test verbatim from end/divers_dozen — an elytra in the chest slot plus flags.is_flying — hung on minecraft:location, the periodic player trigger BACAP uses in potion/marine_marauder, so it resolves on the first 20-tick sample after take-off. The 'within a minute' clause is dropped: criteria cannot relate two events in time and the detector budget went elsewhere. Earned by the receiver, which is why the description is written from their side.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Act of God** — One criterion, the same trigger BACAP nether/electrifying_alliance uses to smite a ghast, with the victim predicate narrowed to a player at distance min 1 so you cannot count yourself. Channeling only works in a thunderstorm, so the weather clause needs no separate check. The description was changed from 'kill' to 'call lightning down onto': vanilla's lightning damage source carries no attacker - only the CHANNELED_LIGHTNING trigger receives the trident's owner - so player_killed_entity would never fire for a lightning kill and killing_blow.direct_entity would not match a lightning_bolt either. Asking for the kill would have made this unobtainable.
- **Aerial Superiority** — One criterion, no fallback and no requirement group: both the killer and the victim must have is_fall_flying set, the flag BACAP itself tests in weaponry/arrow_of_ultimate_obliteration. The trigger fires from LivingEntity.die before anything clears the victim's gliding flag, so the flag is still set when it is evaluated. The earlier equipment fallback was deleted outright: it accepted a victim who was merely off the ground, which any jump satisfies, and it made this criterion dead code.
- **Arise, Sir Loin** — Pure criteria, no function. The knighting blow is damage.taken between 0.01 and 0.99 - 'taken' rather than 'dealt' on purpose, because 'under half a heart' means the health the victim actually loses after armour, which is what makes this achievable at all: a Golden Sword's raw 4 damage can never be pushed under 1, but a well-armoured target absorbing it down to a fraction of a heart can. 'taken' is attested in weaponry/bow_spammer. The victim must be a player, sneaking (flags.is_sneaking, the shape used in technical/air_battle_detect), and at least 1 block away, because player_hurt_entity has no self-exclusion. The knight's own regalia is an entity_properties equipment predicate on mainhand and head, matching the equipment shape in enchanting/silent_but_deadly.
- **Arms Dealer** — Pure criteria, AND-ed, both native PvP. 'armed' fires on the giver when another player picks up their thrown sword (recipient at distance >= 1, so retrieving your own drop does not count). 'slain' is minecraft:entity_killed_player — BACAP's nether/et_tu_brute shape — with the killer required to be a player holding a sword in mainhand; equipment on the killed-by entity predicate is BACAP's own usage from monsters/custom_boss_fight. Deliberately not claimed: that the sword that killed you is the sword you gave away. Stamping donor data into custom_data on the item, as the mechanism note suggested, needs a detector, and the pair of criteria already makes this impossible without a second player twice over.
- **Bare Knuckle Boxing** — Criterion plus detector. The 'kill' criterion is native PvP with a minecraft:is_player_attack killing_blow tag (the lancelot shape), so only a melee blow arms it; the impossible guard sits in its own requirements group, an AND with 'kill', so the criterion alone can never complete the advancement. bucket_fast then picks up any player showing kill=true,impossible=false - the same latch idiom redstone/trapped uses - and detect/bare_knuckle consumes the latch with 'advancement revoke ... only ... kill' FIRST, so a failed check cannot leave it set and re-fire every tick, then grants only if 'if items' finds nothing in container.*, any of the four armour slots or weapon.offhand. Because the fast bucket runs the tick after the kill and death drops carry a pickup delay, a killer who was empty-handed at the moment of the kill is still empty-handed on the check. Scope honestly narrowed from the original idea: only the KILLER's emptiness is verified. The victim is dead by the time anything can look at them, no entity predicate can test an empty inventory, and post-mortem their inventory is meaningless anyway, so 'you are both carrying nothing' became 'you are carrying nothing'.
- **Blood Debt** — Function-granted but WITHOUT a new detection function: three lines in the medium bucket read the bacaptmh_ekill and bacaptmh_edeath flags that the pack's existing detect/f2_events already stamps ungated from consumed playerKillCount and deathCount mirrors, so no lifetime total can be read as a fresh kill. Line one grants to a fresh killer whose freshly-dead neighbour within 64 blocks still carries a bounty; line two then stamps a 120-window (60-second) bounty on any fresh killer standing over a fresh corpse; line three ages the bounty. Order matters - the payout is evaluated before the new bounty is written, so a killer can never collect on the bounty they just earned themselves. Documented weakening from the spare: the killer's identity is not carried anywhere a command can compare, so 'the player who killed YOUR teammate' is approximated by pairing a kill flag with a death flag inside 64 blocks, and the minimum is 2 players rather than 3.
- **Butch and Sundance** — Modelled directly on BACAP weaponry/hit_and_run: player_killed_entity, killing_blow tagged #minecraft:is_projectile, and mainhand/offhand variants ORed in a single requirements group. The multiplayer half is the vehicle predicate — a #minecraft:boat whose passenger list contains a player at distance min 0.3, which rejects the shooter himself (distance 0) and admits the second rider (~0.8 blocks away). Mechanism substituted from the idea: two coupled minecarts cannot be expressed in criteria, and the minecart version would also have collided with BACAP's own Hit and Run; the two-in-a-boat form keeps the outlaw-duo framing, is airtight two-player, and needs no function.
- **Catch of the Day** — Pure criteria, the exact shape BACAP uses for weaponry/indiana_jones with the hooked entity narrowed to minecraft:player. fishing_rod_hooked fires on retrieval, so it only pays out once the hooked player is actually reeled in, and a rod cannot hook its own user.
- **Cover Fire** — No detector. The criterion is BACAP monsters/anti_air's long-range projectile kill (is_projectile killing blow plus an absolute distance floor on the victim) with the `targeted_entity` field Enhanced Discoveries uses in adventure/save_me bolted on: the monster must currently be hunting a player. Both distances are measured from the shooter, so `targeted_entity` at min 1 excludes the shooter himself - if the mob were hunting the sniper, that distance would be 0 and the criterion would not fire. The health clause is enforced separately in the FAST bucket (once per tick, because a player at two hearts does not stay there): the grant needs a non-spectator 1..80 blocks from the sniper sitting at 3 or less on the declared bacaptmh_m1hp health objective. The rescued player is granted first, then the sniper, then the hook is revoked. The description was reworded from 'the mob that is attacking' to 'a monster that is hunting' because it is the mob's target, not its last swing, that is checked.
- **Duel at Dawn** — Native PvP damage, no function. player_hurt_entity fires on the shooter; the victim must be a player between 9 and 11 blocks away, which both enforces the ten paces and self-excludes — player_hurt_entity has no built-in self-exclusion and an arrow that lands back on its own archer would otherwise satisfy it, but that arrives at distance 0. The projectile is pinned with damage.type.direct_entity on #minecraft:arrows, the tag Enhanced Discoveries uses for the same purpose. Sunrise is enforced with the minecraft:time_check loot condition in the criterion's `player` list — clock minecraft:overworld, period 24000, window 0..600 — copying Enhanced Discoveries' The Beginning of the Day, which establishes that daytime tick 0 is 6:00. The dimension is pinned to the overworld so the clock means something. Note this uses the time_check loot condition rather than a `time query`, so none of the daytime/gametime parsing traps apply.
- **Falling Out** — Three independent facts have to line up, and each one is now proved rather than guessed. (1) The dropper: placing an anvil fires the dropped hook, which arms him for ten seconds and is revoked again immediately, so it stays a dated event. (2) The cause of death: the crushed_* criteria are minecraft:entity_hurt_player with damage.blocked false and a source_entity that is a falling_block carrying an anvil BlockState - the exact criterion BACAP uses for enchanting/classic_cartoon_comedy - so an anvil provably struck the victim unblocked. Standing under an anvil and dying of lava no longer counts. (3) The attribution: detect/anvil_stamp runs at each live falling anvil, takes the single nearest armed player within 12 blocks and writes that one player's permanent id onto everyone within 2.5 blocks of the anvil, so the payout names one player instead of being broadcast to everyone within 200 blocks who has been building with anvils. The payout fires when a stamped player's death flag comes up in the same cycle as a crushed hook, and grants only the player whose id matches the stamp; the victim is tagged for the length of that command and excluded from the selector, so dropper and victim can never be the same player and this cannot be completed alone. The impossible criterion in its own AND group keeps the advancement from ever completing by itself. Two deliberate compromises: the crushed hooks are revoked after every read but only for players who do not already hold the advancement - revoking a criterion of a finished advancement would strip it off them - so a victim who already holds Falling Out is judged on the stamp alone, which is what keeps him usable as somebody else's victim on a two-player world instead of making it unobtainable there; and hurt and death are paired at the 10-tick resolution of the bucket, so a player who survives an anvil and dies of something else inside the same tenth of a second while still standing under a live falling anvil would also pay out.
- **First Blood** — Pure criteria: player_killed_entity fires on the killer when the entity killed is of type minecraft:player, so any PvP kill by any means grants it, and only the killer is granted. A player cannot be the entity of his own player_killed_entity, so it can never be earned alone. requirements is left empty so the default (the single criterion) applies.
- **Flawless Victory** — minecraft:player_killed_entity on a player with an nbt predicate of {Health:20.0f} on the killer - the same NbtPredicate BACAP uses on entities in enchanting/classic_cartoon_comedy, read here off the player's own save data. The fight-window state machine in the original idea would have cost a detection function and needed proximity guesswork to attribute hits, so 'they never landed one on you' is expressed as 'you are still untouched at the moment of the kill'. Two caveats: regenerating back to full before the finishing blow also passes, and any max-health modifier on the server makes 20.0f the wrong number.
- **Head of the Household** — inventory_changed on minecraft:player_head, in the shape of vanilla's dragon_breath criterion, ANDed with player_hurt_entity on another player carrying the distance-min-1 guard (which player_hurt_entity needs, having no self-exclusion). A player head has exactly one survival source — a Charged Creeper explosion killing a player — so obtaining one already implies the creeper half without a detector. The AND with 'you have damaged another player' is what makes it strictly multiplayer, since a lone player could otherwise blow themselves up with a charged creeper and pick up their own head. What the pack cannot see is whose head it is or that you lured the creeper, so the two criteria are independent latches rather than one linked event.
- **Highway Robbery** — One criterion. The pickup is thrown_item_picked_up_by_player on Emeralds with the diamonds_to_you thrower guard (entity_type player, distance min 1, self-excluding at 0). The loadout is proved in two shipped idioms rather than one guessed one: an equipment mainhand item predicate exactly as BACAP weaponry/poseidon_vs_hades.json writes it, and the loaded-crossbow NBT match copied verbatim from BACAP weaponry/explosive_fire.json with minecraft:arrow substituted for the firework. I deliberately did not put charged_projectiles inside the mainhand item predicate's 'predicates' map: no file in either reference pack does that, so the sub-predicate's exact codec shape would have been a guess, and a wrong field name stops the whole file loading. The honest cost of the safe route is that the two checks are independent - you are holding a crossbow, and you own an arrow-loaded crossbow, without proof they are the same one. Requires an arrow specifically, not a firework.
- **King of the Hill** — The 'hurt' criterion (minecraft:entity_hurt_player with damage.source_entity an entity_type minecraft:player at distance >= 1, the structure BACAP uses in end/dragon_shield) is treated as an EVENT, not a latch: detect/king_of_the_hill consumes it into a 200-tick contest window and revokes it in the same pass, so the defender has to be hit again every ten seconds or the window lapses and the timer stops. Requirements are two AND-ed groups, [[hurt],[impossible]], so latching hurt can never complete the advancement by itself. Each fast tick the function reads Pos[0] and Pos[2] with 'execute store result ... run data get', which truncates to a stable one-block cell; leaving that cell resets the timer to zero. Y is deliberately not part of the key because knockback lifts the defender constantly and keying on it would reset every run. The timer only advances while at least two other non-spectator, non-creative players are within 12 blocks (distance=0.5.. excludes the defender without an identity test), the contest window is live, and blazeandcave:is_on_ground holds. At 1200 fast passes — one real minute — everyone within 16 blocks is granted, defender and challengers alike.
- **Living Will** — Five handovers in five AND groups; each item predicate carries an enchantments sub-predicate with a levels min, copied from BACAP's enchanting/master_tridenteer, so a bare netherite piece will not do — the whole estate has to be Protection IV and the Elytra has to carry Mending. Rewritten from the original: snapshotting a player's full inventory, tracking each stack to one of four distinct receivers, watching a death and then reassembling the set is several hundred lines of function and four separate UUID tallies, and no spare in the pool covered it, so the title and the bequest survived and the mechanism became a pure-criteria one. That drops it from five players to two, and the pack cannot see that the giver ends up empty-handed; the cost of the kit, not a death, is what carries the tier.
- **Long Shot Rivalry** — Pure criteria built from BACAP's own long-range kill shapes: killing_blow requires a projectile whose direct entity is an arrow carrying weapon:{id:"minecraft:bow"} (the same NBT test weaponry/the_mighty_hunter uses to exclude crossbows), and the victim must be a player at least 100 blocks away horizontally, the same distance form as weaponry/king_of_the_sea. requirements is empty so the single criterion applies.
- **Mutually Assured Destruction** — Detection is a function, but the statistic mirrors it reads are maintained by bacaptmh:detect/f2_events, which runs every 10 ticks gated on nothing at all: the playerKillCount and deathCount mirrors are converted into the shared three-cycle flags bacaptmh_ekill / bacaptmh_edeath and then zeroed there and nowhere else, so no mirror can ever go stale behind a closed gate and no other advancement consumes them. detect/mutual_destruction grants any player holding both flags who has a second player holding both flags within 64 blocks - the flags are kept for every player, including those who already hold the advancement, so a partner can never be locked out. It proves a mutual trade only to the extent that two players each killed a player and each died within the same 1.5 s and stood within 64 blocks of each other.
- **Ransom Note** — Six criteria, all six in their own AND groups. 'took_a_hostage' is player_killed_entity with entity_type minecraft:player, which is native on this server because PvP is on and needs no detector; ServerPlayer.awardKillScore skips the trigger when the killed entity is the killer, so it cannot be self-satisfied. The five give-backs are thrown_item_picked_up_by_entity - BACAP mining/diamonds_to_you, 'throw diamonds at another player' - each pinned to one Diamond piece with entity_type player and the distance min 1 guard on the receiver, which self-excludes because picking your own drop back up measures you against yourself at distance 0. The spare's real request, tracking the victim's dropped item entities by UUID and reconciling them inside 1200 ticks, was dropped: death drops are created by Inventory.dropAll with includeThrowerName false, so they carry no thrower at all and the pickup trigger never fires for them - that is why the returned gear has to be handed over deliberately rather than scavenged. Consequences of that: the criteria do not verify the five pieces went back to the same player you killed, nor that they are the gear they lost, and nothing is timed. The description says 'a full set', not 'their set'.
- **Rest In Pieces** — Two AND-ed criteria: a PvP kill (player_killed_entity on entity_type player — native, no function) and a Wither Rose placement (placed_block with a location_check on the placed block, the structure BACAP uses in Extrabiologist and Target Acquired). The kill half is what makes it impossible alone. Substitution note: the original wanted the rose on the exact death block, which needs a death-position store and a second detector; the honest version pairs the two acts without proving the coordinates match, so a player could in principle plant the rose somewhere else. Requirements are two separate groups, so both must happen — not one group, which would be an OR and would let a lone player finish it by planting a rose.
- **Rope-a-Dope** — DETECTOR 4 of 5. `blocked` is entity_hurt_player with damage.blocked true — the shape BACAP uses in end/shouldnt_my_shield_levitate_too — and source_entity pinned to entity_type player with the distance absolute min 1 guard, which matters here because entity_hurt_player has no self-exclusion and an arrow you fired that comes back down on your own shield would otherwise count. The impossible criterion sits in its own requirements group. bucket_fast runs detect/rope_a_dope for anyone holding {blocked=true,impossible=false}: it revokes the advancement (consuming the flag, so it can only be re-set by the next real block), adds one to bacaptmh_rope, and grants at 20. The streak is reset in bucket_medium for any player whose bacaptmh_dmgd damage-dealt delta is positive — that delta is already computed ungated for everyone by the existing detect/sample earlier in the same bucket. Honest limitations: the counter does not track WHICH opponent, so twenty blocks split across two attackers still count, and 'without hitting back' is enforced as 'without dealing any damage to anything', mobs included, which is stricter than the original wording.
- **Second Wind** — Pure criteria, no detector. 'burn' is minecraft:used_totem (Enhanced Discoveries technical/used_totem). 'finish' is player_killed_entity on a player - native PvP, and no distance guard is used or wanted here because you cannot be your own killed entity and a melee kill often happens under one block. The 'still under the totem' half is carried by the totem's own effect signature: Regeneration II (amplifier at least 1) and Fire Resistance together, which a totem grants for 45 s and 40 s, plus an entity_scores read of bacaptmh_hp, a vanilla health objective, at 10 or below - five hearts. requirements are [[burn],[finish]], an AND of groups. Honest limitation: the two criteria are independent, so the totem need not have been burned on a blow from the player you then kill, nor in the same fight; a brewed Regeneration II plus a Fire Resistance potion can also produce the effect pair.
- **Secret Handshake** — Two criteria ANDed: you hurt a player (player_hurt_entity, entity_type player, distance min 1) and a player hurt you (entity_hurt_player with a source_entity predicate, the shape BACAP's Falling Out uses), both capped at 1.0 damage dealt, which is a bare fist. 'gave' is unsatisfiable alone, so the pair is impossible alone even though entity_hurt_player has no self-exclusion of its own; the distance min 1 on source_entity is there anyway so that your own arrow coming back down cannot arm 'took'. Substituted mechanism: the original wanted the two punches on the exact same tick, which needs a fast-bucket pairing function. Same-tick was dropped to stay inside the detector budget - this is a trade of punches, not a simultaneous one. The 1.0 cap can in principle also be met by a heavily cooldown-nerfed weapon swing or a hit on very high armour.
- **Shield Wall** — The damage_blocked_by_shield statistic only moves when a raised shield actually stops damage, so it doubles as 'blocking while being hit'. bacaptmh:detect/f2_events turns that mirror into the one-second bacaptmh_eblock flag for every player, gated on nothing, so the mirror can never go stale and holders of the advancement keep being flagged and keep counting towards other players' walls. detect/shield_wall then counts, for each flagged player, the flagged players within 8 blocks of him (himself included) and grants everyone who reaches three. The bucket entry counts players properly - scoreboard players set #f2players 0 then one add per non-spectator - instead of the @a[limit=3] form, which only ever tests for one player.
- **Snowball War** — Snowballs deal zero damage to players and Player.hurt returns early on zero damage, so no damage trigger ever fires; the projectile is watched instead. The watch starts from a player - bacaptmh:detect/f2_near runs as each non-spectator and looks for snowballs within 48 blocks of him - because a bare @e in a tick function only sees the dimension of the server command source, i.e. the overworld, and because an @e with no distance bound walks every loaded entity. Each snowball is then taken 'at @s on origin if entity @s[type=minecraft:player]', so the chain runs as the thrower and snow golem, dispenser and Breeze projectiles are skipped entirely; reaching the same snowball from two players in one tick is harmless because every step below is idempotent. The thrower carries a temporary owner tag while victims are picked, so he can never be one of his own three. Distinctness is now kept on the thrower, not on the victim: every player is given a permanent id at first sight and the thrower remembers the ids of the three players he has already hit in this window, so a second thrower can no longer overwrite the first thrower's record and let one player be counted twice. Every player inside the radius is offered rather than only the nearest, so an already-counted player standing in front no longer hides an uncounted one. The ten-second window is opened once, on the first hit, and is not pushed forward by later hits. No score is ever written to a snowball entity, so nothing accumulates in scoreboard.dat. Because the tick function runs before entities move, the last sample of a snowball is up to one tick of travel short of its impact, so the 1.6 block radius is what makes a real hit register; a snowball passing that close without landing also counts.
- **Sparring Partner** — Pure criteria, no function. Condition structure copied verbatim from blazeandcave:enchanting/overkill (damage.dealt with an is_player_attack / !mace_smash / !blazeandcave:spear type tag set), with the target narrowed to minecraft:player. 'dealt' is the raw damage handed to the hurt call, before armour and before any clamp against remaining health, so a 20 HP target does not cap it - a crit from a Sharpness V netherite axe under Strength clears 20 comfortably. The distance absolute min 1 guard is the mining/diamonds_to_you idiom: player_hurt_entity has no self-exclusion, and a projectile or explosion returning to its thrower is at distance 0.
- **Spear Joust** — Pure criteria in BACAP's own monsters/lancelot and weaponry/hit_and_run shape, with no statistic mirror. The blazeandcave:spear damage type tag is the Spear charge attack, the victim must be a player whose vehicle is in #blazeandcave:all_horses, and the attacker must be holding a Spear from #blazeandcave:spears while riding a horse himself - so the blow is proved to have landed on a specific mounted player instead of being inferred from two riders damaging anything nearby. Mainhand and offhand are two criteria in one OR group, exactly as BACAP writes it. player_hurt_entity has no self-exclusion, but a Spear is a melee weapon - blazeandcave:spear resolves to the minecraft:spear damage type and there is no thrown spear entity - so unlike a trident it cannot come back down on its own user, and no distance guard is needed to keep this off a solo player.
- **Trident Tag** — The two criteria are hooks, not the completion condition: the third criterion is minecraft:impossible and the requirements are three separate AND groups, so the advancement can never complete on its own. bacaptmh:detect/trident_tag reads the hooks every 10 ticks and clears each one again with 'advancement revoke ... <criterion>' so it can fire afresh, which is what turns a permanent criterion into a dated event; the hit pass runs before the catch pass, so a hit and a catch landing in the same cycle still pair up. hit_player requires a projectile whose direct entity is a trident entity striking a player, which excludes a melee trident stab (its direct entity is the player), and it now carries entity.distance.absolute.min = 1, the self-guard BACAP uses in mining/diamonds_to_you: player_hurt_entity has no self-exclusion, so without it a trident thrown straight up and caught on the head satisfied the hook at distance 0 and one player could finish this alone. Any real hit on another player is at least a block away. It opens a four-second window; if the caught hook lands inside that window the advancement is granted and the window is cleared. Both hooks and the grant belong to the same player, so the revoke is only ever applied to players who do not hold the advancement and nobody can be locked out. The remaining looseness is on the catch side and is left deliberately: caught is 'an inventory change while a Loyalty trident is in the inventory', which a returning Loyalty trident always produces but which an unrelated item move within four seconds of a genuine hit also produces. Every tighter form was worse - a returning trident goes through Inventory.add and lands in the first free slot, not necessarily a hand, so an equipment predicate would make the advancement miss real catches, and a count-before/count-after check cannot be snapshotted before the return when both hooks are read in the same cycle. It over-grants a player who really did spear another player and is carrying a Loyalty trident; it is not solo-completable.
- **Wind Duel** — A wind burst deals no damage, so the projectile is watched. detect/f2_near runs as each non-spectator and looks for minecraft:wind_charge entities within 48 blocks of him - starting from a player instead of a bare @e is what makes the watch work outside the overworld and keeps the scan bounded - then takes each charge 'at @s on origin if entity @s[type=minecraft:player]', so a Breeze projectile (minecraft:breeze_wind_charge, a different entity type) and a dispenser-fired charge (no origin at all) are never followed. Credit is paired, not broadcast: every player carries a permanent id, the launched player records the id of the thrower who marked him, and the payout grants only the player whose id matches that record. The thrower carries an owner tag while victims are picked, so a self-launch is impossible, and creative, spectator and Elytra-gliding players are never marked. The rise check runs every tick, so the top of the arc cannot be missed. Two things make 'rose six blocks' mean 'was launched': the window is one second, not three - long enough for the burst's arc, too short to climb six blocks by jumping up a block stack, by ladder or by rocket - and the gliding test is re-applied every tick inside the window instead of only at marking time, so deploying an Elytra after being marked cancels the window rather than farming it. Heights are read as hundredths of a block (Pos[1] scaled by 100, threshold 600) because a plain read truncates towards zero and would misreport the rise by up to a whole block below Y=0. Six blocks is what a wind charge under a player's feet actually delivers; the original twenty blocks was more than the burst can produce, so that version would silently never have fired.

</details>
